\sum_{k\neq j}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{k}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m}{\pi_{j}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}B^{-4}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.

(17)

•

For
\forall i\in S_{l}
,

0\leq\pi_{i}(\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{l}^{*}\right\rangle^{2})\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{md}{\pi_{l}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}B^{-4}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.

(18)

•

For
\forall j\in[m]
,

\left|\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}-\pi_{j}^{*}\right|\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m^{2}n}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{3}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.

(19)

•

For
\forall j\in[m]
,

\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}-\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\right|\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{mnd}}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{5/4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3/2}D_{\max}^{9/2}\right){{\varepsilon}}^{1/4}.

(20)

•

For
\forall j\in[m]
,

\left\|\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}-\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|_{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m^{2}nd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-7/2}D_{\max}^{10}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.

(21)

Now we convert them back to
{\bm{\mu}}_{i}
, and give the proof for the main identifiability result (Theorem
B.1
):

Proof of Theorem
B.1
.

Now we have proved every property needed for
\{{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\}_{i\in[n]}
(condition (
15
) - (
21
)), and can convert them back to properties of
\{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\}_{i\in[n]}
.

Proof of (
6
).

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}\right\|^{2}=\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}\left(W^{\top}\right)^{-1}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}-\left(W^{\top}\right)^{-1}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}=\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\|\left(W^{\top}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}-{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{\max}\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}-{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}{\leq}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{7/2}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}D_{\max}^{10}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}},

where we used (
29
) and
\lambda_{\max}\leq D_{\max}
in the last inequality.

Proof of (
7
).
This is already proven in (
19
).

Proof of (
8
).
This is a direct corollary of (
6
) and (
7
). Since
{\varepsilon}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\pi_{\min}^{*^{8}}}{m^{4}n^{2}}\lambda_{\min}^{4}D_{\max}^{-12}\right)
, (
7
) implies

\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\geq\pi_{j}^{*}-\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m^{2}n}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{3}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\geq\pi_{j}^{*}-\frac{1}{4}\pi_{\min}^{*}\geq\frac{3}{4}\pi_{j}^{*}.

On the other hand, by (
6
) we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in S_{j}\setminus S_{j}(\delta_{close})}\pi_{i}\leq\frac{1}{\delta^{2}}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{7/2}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}D_{\max}^{10}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\leq\frac{1}{4}\pi_{\min}^{*}.

Therefore

\sum_{i\in S_{j}(\delta_{close})}\pi_{i}=\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}-\sum_{i\in S_{j}\setminus S_{j}(\delta_{close})}\pi_{i}\geq\frac{1}{2}\pi_{j}^{*}.

Proof of (
9
).

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\pi_{j}^{*}{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}\right\|=\left\|\left(W^{\top}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}-{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{\max}^{1/2}\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}-{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\|\overset{\eqref{eq: thm 32 linear term}}{\leq}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m^{2}nd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-7/2}D_{\max}^{21/2}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.

∎

B.4.1
Omitted proofs

We give the omitted proofs in this section.
See
B.7

Proof.

We show these one by one based on Lemma
B.6
.

Proof of (
15
).

For any
{\bm{v}}\in R^{d}
, we know that
\lambda_{\min}^{-1/2}\left\|{\bm{v}}\right\|\geq\left\|W^{\top}{\bm{v}}\right\|=\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\Sigma^{-1/2}&0\\
0&\lambda_{\min}^{-1/2}I_{d-m}\end{pmatrix}V^{\top}{\bm{v}}\right\|_{2}\geq\lambda_{\max}^{-1/2}\left\|{\bm{v}}\right\|
. Since
D_{\min}\geq 2B
, we have for
\forall l\in[m],i\in[n]
,

\mathcal{O}\left(D_{\max}\right)\geq\left\|{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\right\|\geq D_{\min}-B\geq B\Rightarrow\mathcal{O}\left(\lambda_{\min}^{-1/2}D_{\max}\right)\geq\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\geq\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{\max}^{-1/2}D_{\min}\geq\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{\max}^{-1/2}B.

Proof of (
16
) and (
17
).

Since
WW^{\top}=V\begin{pmatrix}\Sigma^{-1}&0\\
0&\lambda_{\min}^{-1}I_{d-m}\end{pmatrix}V^{\top}
, we know that the maximum and minimum eigenvalues of
WW^{\top}
are
1/\lambda_{\min}
and
1/\lambda_{\max}
, respectively. Then for any
l\in[m],i\in S_{l}
, by
D_{\min}\geq 2\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}}B
we have

\displaystyle{{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*}}^{\top}WW^{\top}{{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*}}\geq\frac{1}{\lambda_{\max}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*}\right\|^{2}\geq\frac{D_{\min}}{\lambda_{\max}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*}\right\|

\displaystyle\geq 2\frac{\lambda_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}}B\cdot\frac{1}{\lambda_{\max}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*}\right\|\geq 2\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*}\right\|\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*}\right\|\geq 2{({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*})}^{\top}WW^{\top}{{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*}}.

Then by (
15
) we have
\forall i\in S_{l}

\begin{split}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{l}^{*}\right\rangle&=\sqrt{\pi_{l}^{*}}\left\langle W^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}_{i},W^{\top}{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*}\right\rangle=\sqrt{\pi_{l}^{*}}{{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*}}^{\top}WW^{\top}{{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*}}+\sqrt{\pi_{l}^{*}}{({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*})}^{\top}WW^{\top}{{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*}}\\
&\geq\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\pi_{l}^{*}}{{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*}}^{\top}WW^{\top}{{\bm{\mu}}_{l}^{*}}=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\pi_{l}^{*}}}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{l}^{*}\right\|^{2}\geq 2\sqrt{\pi_{l}^{*}}\lambda_{\max}^{-1}B^{2}.\end{split}

(22)

Now we look at the
(j,j,k,k)
entry of the
4
th order tensor decomposition error (
j,k\in[m],j\neq k
) as

\displaystyle m\mathcal{O}\left(\lambda_{\min}^{-2}D_{\max}^{4}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\geq m\left\|T_{4}\right\|_{2}\geq\left|\left\langle\sum_{l}\frac{1}{\pi_{l}^{*}}{{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{l}}^{*^{\otimes 4}}-\sum_{i}{\pi_{i}}{{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}}^{\otimes 4},\sum_{k\neq j}{{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}}^{*^{\otimes 2}}\otimes{{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{k}}^{*^{\otimes 2}}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in[n]}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\left(\sum_{k\neq j}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{k}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right)\geq\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\left(\sum_{k\neq j}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{k}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq: tvmu projection bound}}{\geq}4{\pi_{j}^{*}}\lambda_{\max}^{-2}B^{4}\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\sum_{k\neq j}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{k}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right),

where the second inequality is due to the definition of spectral norm, and the third identity is because
{{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{l}}^{*^{\otimes 4}}
is orthogonal with
{{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}}^{*^{\otimes 2}}\otimes{{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{k}}^{*^{\otimes 2}},\forall l,j\neq k
.

Note that
\lambda_{\max}=\left\|M_{2}\right\|\leq D_{\max}
, so for any
i\in S_{j}
we have

\sum_{k\neq j}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{k}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m}{\pi_{j}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}B^{-4}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.

Proof of (
18
).

Recall that
\{{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}^{*}\}_{i\in[m]}
form an orthonormal basis for an
m
-dimensional subspace of
\mathbf{R}^{d}
, so if we define matrix
\tilde{U}_{*}\in\mathbf{R}^{d\times m}
as
\tilde{U}_{*}\coloneqq\begin{pmatrix}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{1}^{*},\cdots,{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{m}^{*}\end{pmatrix}
, then there exists a unitary matrix
\tilde{U}\in\mathbf{R}^{d\times d}
such that

\tilde{U}\coloneqq\begin{pmatrix}\tilde{U}_{*},\tilde{U}_{\bot}\end{pmatrix},

where
\tilde{U}_{\bot}^{\top}\in\mathbf{R}^{m\times d}
is the projection matrix onto the orthogonal complement space of
span({\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{1}^{*},\ldots,{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{m}^{*})
.

Since
\tilde{U}_{\bot}^{\top}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{l}^{*}=\bm{0},\forall l\in[m]
, we know that

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\lambda_{\min}^{-1}D_{\max}^{2}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\geq\left\|\mathcal{T}_{2}\right\|_{2}{\geq}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\left\|\mathcal{T}_{2}\right\|_{F}\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\left\|\tilde{U}_{\bot}^{\top}\left(\sum_{l}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{l}^{*}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{l}^{*^{\top}}-\sum_{i}\pi_{i}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}^{{\top}}\right)\tilde{U}_{\bot}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\left\|\sum_{i}\pi_{i}\left(\tilde{U}_{\bot}^{\top}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right)\left(\tilde{U}_{\bot}^{\top}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right\|_{F}\geq\frac{1}{{d}}tr\left(\sum_{i}\pi_{i}\left(\tilde{U}_{\bot}^{\top}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right)\left(\tilde{U}_{\bot}^{\top}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right)=\frac{1}{d}\sum_{i\in[n]}\pi_{i}\left\|\tilde{U}_{\bot}^{\top}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}.

Rearranging term yields
\sum_{i\in[n]}\pi_{i}\left\|\tilde{U}_{\bot}^{\top}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(d\lambda_{\min}^{-1}D_{\max}^{2}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}
.

By Pythagorean theorem, for
i\in S_{l}
we know that

\begin{split}&0\leq\pi_{i}(\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{l}^{*}\right\rangle^{2})=\sum_{j\neq l}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}+\pi_{i}\left\|\tilde{U}_{\bot}^{\top}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}\\
&\overset{\eqref{eq: thm 32 angle bound}}{\leq}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m}{\pi_{l}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}B^{-4}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}+\mathcal{O}\left(d\lambda_{\min}^{-1}D_{\max}^{2}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{md}{\pi_{l}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}B^{-4}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.\end{split}

Proof of (
19
), (
20
).

Now we look at the diagonal entries in
\mathcal{T}_{k}
,
k\in\{2,3,4\}
. For
\forall j\in[m]

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\lambda_{\min}^{-k/2}D_{\max}^{k}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\geq\left\|\mathcal{T}_{k}\right\|_{2}\geq\left|\left\langle\sum_{l\in[m]}{\pi_{l}^{*}}^{1-k/2}{{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{l}}^{*^{\otimes k}}-\sum_{i\in[n]}\pi_{i}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}^{\otimes k},{{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}}^{*^{\otimes k}}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=\left|{\pi_{j}^{*}}^{1-k/2}-\sum_{i\in[n]}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{k}\right|\geq\left|{\pi_{j}^{*}}^{1-k/2}-\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{k}\right|-\sum_{i\notin S_{j}}\pi_{i}|\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle|^{k}.

By (
17
) we have
\forall k\in\{2,3,4\}
,

\pi_{i}|\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle|^{k}\leq\left(\pi_{i}|\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle|^{2}\right)^{k/2}\leq\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}B^{-4}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.

So for
\forall j\in[m],k\in\{2,3,4\}
,

\left|{\pi_{j}^{*}}^{1-k}-\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{k}\right|=\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*^{k/2}}}\left|{\pi_{j}^{*}}^{1-k/2}-\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{k}\right|\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mn}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{3}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.

(23)

Furthermore, for
i\in S_{j}
, by Bernoulli inequality we know that

\begin{split}&\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{k}=\pi_{i}\left(\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}-(\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2})\right)^{k/2}\geq\pi_{i}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{k}-\frac{k}{2}\pi_{i}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{k-2}(\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}).\end{split}

Then note that
\forall i\in[n],\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\leq\left\|W^{\top}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\right\|\leq\lambda_{\min}^{-1/2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\right\|\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\lambda_{\min}^{-1/2}D_{\max}\right)
, combined with (
18
) we have

\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{k}\geq\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{k}\geq\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{k}-\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\frac{k}{2}\pi_{i}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{k-2}(\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2})\\
&\geq\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{k}-\mathcal{O}\left(\lambda_{\min}^{-1}D^{2}_{\max}\right)\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}(\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2})\\
&\geq\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{k}-\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}B^{-4}D_{\max}^{8}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.\end{split}

Combining the above with (
23
), we get for
\forall j\in[m],k\in\{2,3,4\}
,

\begin{split}&\left|{\pi_{j}^{*}}^{1-k/2}-\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{k}\right|\leq\left|{\pi_{j}^{*}}^{1-k/2}-\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{k}\right|+\left|\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{k}-\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{k}\right|\\
&\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mn}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}B^{-4}D_{\max}^{8}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}D_{\max}^{8}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.\end{split}

(24)

Next we will repeatedly apply Lemma
B.8
to (
23
) and (
24
).

•

First we apply Lemma
B.8
, (i) to (
23
) as: set
a_{i}=\pi_{i}
,
x_{i}=\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle
,
a=\pi_{j}^{*},x=\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}
. Since
\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mn}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{3}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\leq\mathcal{O}\left({\pi_{\min}^{*}}\right)\leq\mathcal{O}\left(c_{a}c_{x}^{4}\right)
, for
\forall j\in[m]
we have
\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\geq\pi_{j}^{*}-\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mn}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{3}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.

On the other hand,

\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}-\pi_{j}^{*}=-\sum_{l\neq j}\left(\sum_{i\in S_{l}}\pi_{i}-\pi_{l}^{*}\right)\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m^{2}n}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{3}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.

So for
\forall j\in[m]
,

\left|\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}-\pi_{j}^{*}\right|\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m^{2}n}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{3}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.

•

Next we apply Lemma
B.8
, (ii) to (
24
): set
a_{i}=\pi_{i},x_{i}=\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|,a=\pi_{j}^{*},x=\pi_{j}^{*^{-1/2}}
. Since
\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}D_{\max}^{8}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\leq\mathcal{O}\left({\pi_{\min}^{*}}\right)\leq\mathcal{O}\left(c_{a}c_{x}^{4}\right)
,. Here
C_{x}=\pi_{\min}^{*^{-1/2}}\geq\pi_{j}^{*^{-1/2}}
, so Lemma
B.8
implies that
\forall j\in[m],

\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}-\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\right)^{2}\leq\pi_{\min}^{*^{-3/2}}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}D_{\max}^{8}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{5/2}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}D_{\max}^{8}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}},

\Rightarrow\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}-\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\right)^{2}\leq\max_{i\in[n]}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{-2}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{5/2}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}D_{\max}^{8}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{5/2}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}D_{\max}^{9}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.

(25)

Combining above with (
15
) yields
\forall j\in[m],

\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}-\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\right|\leq\sqrt{\left(\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\right)\left(\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}-\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\right)^{2}\right)}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{mnd}}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{5/4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3/2}D_{\max}^{9/2}\right){{\varepsilon}}^{1/4}.\end{split}

Proof of (
21
).

This is the most difficult part. We first look at the third order tensor decomposition. For
j\in[m]
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\lambda_{\min}^{-3/2}D_{\max}^{3}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\geq\left\|\mathcal{T}_{3}\right\|_{2}\geq\left\|\left(\sum_{l\in[m]}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{l}^{*}}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{l}^{*^{\otimes 3}}-\sum_{i\in[n]}\pi_{i}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}^{{\otimes 3}}\right)\left({\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*^{\otimes 2}}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}-\sum_{i\in[n]}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\geq\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}-\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|-\sum_{i\notin S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq: thm 32 angle bound}}{\geq}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}-\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|-\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mn}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-5/2}B^{-4}D_{\max}^{7}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}},

where we used
\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\lambda_{\min}^{-1/2}D_{\max}\right)
in the last inequality.

Therefore

\begin{split}&\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mn}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-5/2}D_{\max}^{7}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\geq\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}-\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\\
&\geq\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}-\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right)\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|.\end{split}

(26)

We now bound the second term above as

\begin{split}&\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right)\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\leq\underbrace{\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right){\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\|}_{I_{1}}+\underbrace{\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left|\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right|\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}-\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|}_{I_{2}}.\end{split}

(27)

To bound the first term
I_{1}
above, note that another bound can be derived from the second order error as

\begin{split}&\mathcal{O}\left(\lambda_{\min}^{-1}D_{\max}^{2}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\geq\left\|\mathcal{T}_{2}\right\|_{2}\geq\left|\left\langle\sum_{l\in[m]}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{l}^{*^{\otimes 2}}-\sum_{i\in[n]}\pi_{i}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}^{{\otimes 2}},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*^{\otimes 2}}\right\rangle\right|=\left|1-\sum_{i\in[n]}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right|\\
&\overset{\eqref{eq: thm 32 angle bound}}{\geq}\left|1-\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right|-\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mn}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}B^{-4}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.\end{split}

(28)

Therefore

\begin{split}&I_{1}=\left|\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right)\right|\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\|\leq\left|\frac{\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}}{\pi_{j}^{*}}-1\right|+\left|1-\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right|\\
&\overset{\eqref{eq: thm 32 pi error}}{\leq}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m^{2}n}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mn}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m^{2}n}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.\end{split}

where we used (
28
) at the start of second line.

Next we bound
I_{2}
as

\displaystyle I_{2}=\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left|\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right|\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}-\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\leq\underbrace{\sqrt{\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left|\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right|^{2}}}_{I_{3}}\cdot\underbrace{\sqrt{\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}-\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}}}_{I_{4}}.

For
I_{3}
we have,

\displaystyle I_{3}^{2}=\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right)^{2}\leq 2\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}-\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}\right)^{2}+2\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq: thm 32 almost parallel}}{\leq}2\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}-\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\right)^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}+\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\right)^{2}+n\mathcal{O}\left(\lambda_{\min}^{-1}D_{\max}^{2}\right)\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{md}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}B^{-4}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{D_{\max}^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}\pi_{\min}^{*}}\right)\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}-\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\right)^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}D_{\max}^{8}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq: thm 33 eq -1}}{\leq}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{7/2}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-4}D_{\max}^{11}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.

For
I_{4}
we have

\begin{split}&I_{4}^{2}={\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}-\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}}=\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}(1+\pi_{j}^{*}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}-2\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\rangle)\\
&=\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|-1\right)^{2}+2\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\rangle\right)\\
&\overset{\eqref{eq: thm 33 eq -1}}{\leq}\pi_{j}^{*}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{5/2}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}D_{\max}^{9}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}+2\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\frac{\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|^{2}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\rangle^{2}}{\left\|{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|+\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\rangle}\\
&\overset{\eqref{eq: thm 32 almost parallel}}{\leq}\pi_{j}^{*}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{5/2}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}D_{\max}^{9}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}+2\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}n\mathcal{O}\left(\lambda_{\max}^{1/2}\right)\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{md}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}B^{-4}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\\
&\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{5/2}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}D_{\max}^{9}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.\end{split}

(29)

Combining the upper bounds for
I_{3}
and
I_{4}
,
I_{2}
can be upper bounded by

I_{2}\leq\sqrt{I_{3}^{2}\cdot I_{4}^{2}}\leq\sqrt{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{7/2}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-4}D_{\max}^{11}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{5/2}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3}D_{\max}^{9}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{3}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-7/2}D_{\max}^{10}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.

Combining the upper bounds for
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
with (
27
), we get
\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right)\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\leq I_{1}+I_{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m^{2}n}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-2}D_{\max}^{6}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mnd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{3}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-7/2}D_{\max}^{10}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m^{2}nd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-7/2}D_{\max}^{10}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.
We plug this back into (
26
) and get the desired bound

\begin{split}&\left\|{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}-\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\leq\pi_{j}^{*}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mn}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-5/2}D_{\max}^{7}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}+{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}}\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\pi_{i}\left(\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}-\left\langle{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i},{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{j}^{*}\right\rangle^{2}\right)\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}}{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}_{i}\right\|\\
&\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{mn}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\lambda_{\min}^{-5/2}D_{\max}^{7}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m^{2}nd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-7/2}D_{\max}^{10}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{m^{2}nd}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-7/2}D_{\max}^{10}\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}.\end{split}

∎

The following technical lemma is used in the proof of Lemma
B.7
.

Lemma B.8
.

(i).
Suppose
a,x,a_{1},\ldots,a_{n}\in\mathbf{R}^{+}
are positive numbers. There exists constants
c_{a},C_{a},c_{x},C_{x}\in\mathbf{R}^{+}
such that
0<c_{a}\leq a\leq C_{a},0<c_{x}\leq x\leq C_{x}
(We assume
c_{a},c_{x}\leq 1\leq C_{a},C_{x}
).
0\leq{\varepsilon}\leq\frac{1}{2}{c_{a}c_{x}^{4}}
is a small number such that

\left|\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{4}-ax^{4}\right|\leq{\varepsilon},

\left|\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{2}-ax^{2}\right|\leq{\varepsilon},

then we have

\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}\geq a-\mathcal{O}\left(c_{x}^{-4}{\varepsilon}\right).

(30)

(ii).
If furthermore the following condition holds

\left|\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{3}-ax^{3}\right|\leq{\varepsilon},

then

\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{2}(x-x_{i})^{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(C_{x}^{3}{\varepsilon}\right).

(31)

Proof.

By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality we have

\left(\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}\right)(ax^{4}+{{\varepsilon}})\geq\left(\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}\right)\left(\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{4}\right)\geq\left(\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{2}\right)^{2}\geq(ax^{2}-{{\varepsilon}})^{2}.

Then we have

\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}\geq\frac{1}{ax^{4}+{{\varepsilon}}}(ax^{2}-{{\varepsilon}})^{2}=a-\frac{2ax{\varepsilon}+a{\varepsilon}-{\varepsilon}^{2}}{ax^{4}+{\varepsilon}}\geq a-\frac{2ax{\varepsilon}+a{\varepsilon}}{ax^{4}}\geq a-\mathcal{O}\left(c_{x}^{-4}{\varepsilon}\right).\end{split}

This proves (
30
). For (
31
) we apply Cauchy-Schwarz again to get

\begin{split}&\left(\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{3}\right)^{2}\leq\left(\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{4}\right)\\
&\leq(ax^{2}+{\varepsilon})(ax^{4}+{\varepsilon})\leq a^{2}x^{6}+\mathcal{O}\left(a(x^{2}+x^{4}){\varepsilon}\right)\leq\left(\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{3}\right)^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(a(x^{2}+x^{4}){\varepsilon}\right),\end{split}

note that we used
{\varepsilon}\leq\frac{1}{2}{c_{a}c_{x}^{4}}\Rightarrow{\varepsilon}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(ax^{k}\right),\forall k\leq 4
.

We define two vectors
{\bm{\alpha}}\coloneqq\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{a_{1}}x_{1}\\
\vdots\\
\sqrt{a_{n}}x_{n}\end{pmatrix},{\bm{\beta}}\coloneqq\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{a_{1}}x_{1}^{2}\\
\vdots\\
\sqrt{a_{n}}x_{n}^{2}\end{pmatrix}.
Then the above inequality implies that
\left|\left\langle{\bm{\alpha}},{\bm{\beta}}\right\rangle^{2}-\left\|{\bm{\alpha}}\right\|^{2}\left\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|^{2}\right|\leq\mathcal{O}\left(a(x^{2}+x^{4}){\varepsilon}\right)
. Since
\left\langle{\bm{\alpha}},{\bm{\beta}}\right\rangle\geq ax^{3}-{\varepsilon}\geq\frac{ax^{3}}{2}
, we have

\mathcal{O}\left(a(x^{2}+x^{4}){\varepsilon}\right)\geq\left|\left\langle{\bm{\alpha}},{\bm{\beta}}\right\rangle^{2}-\left\|{\bm{\alpha}}\right\|^{2}\left\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|^{2}\right|=\left|\left\langle{\bm{\alpha}},{\bm{\beta}}\right\rangle-\left\|{\bm{\alpha}}\right\|\left\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|\right|\cdot\left(\left\langle{\bm{\alpha}},{\bm{\beta}}\right\rangle+\left\|{\bm{\alpha}}\right\|\left\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|\right)\geq\frac{ax^{3}}{2}\left|\left\langle{\bm{\alpha}},{\bm{\beta}}\right\rangle-\left\|{\bm{\alpha}}\right\|\left\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|\right|.

Then

\left\|\left\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|{\bm{\alpha}}-\left\|{\bm{\alpha}}\right\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|^{2}=2\left\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|\left\|{\bm{\alpha}}\right\|\left(\left\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|\left\|{\bm{\alpha}}\right\|-\left\langle{\bm{\alpha}},{\bm{\beta}}\right\rangle\right)\leq\left\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|\left\|{\bm{\alpha}}\right\|\mathcal{O}\left((x+\frac{1}{x}){\varepsilon}\right).

On the other hand,

\sqrt{a}x+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{a}x}{\varepsilon}\right)\geq\sqrt{ax^{2}+{\varepsilon}}\geq\left\|{\bm{\alpha}}\right\|=\sqrt{\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{2}}\geq\sqrt{ax^{2}-{\varepsilon}}\geq\sqrt{a}{x}-\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{a}x}{\varepsilon}\right),

\sqrt{a}x^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{a}x^{2}}{\varepsilon}\right)\geq\sqrt{ax^{4}+{\varepsilon}}\geq\left\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|=\sqrt{\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{4}}\geq\sqrt{ax^{4}-{\varepsilon}}\geq\sqrt{a}{x^{2}}-\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{a}x^{2}}{\varepsilon}\right).

Therefore

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(a(x^{2}+x^{4}){\varepsilon}\right)\geq\left\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|\left\|{\bm{\alpha}}\right\|\mathcal{O}\left((x+\frac{1}{x}){\varepsilon}\right)\geq\left\|\left\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|{\bm{\alpha}}-\left\|{\bm{\alpha}}\right\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|^{2}=\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{2}\left(\left\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|-\left\|{\bm{\alpha}}\right\|x_{i}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{2}\left(\left(\sqrt{a}x^{2}-\sqrt{a}xx_{i}\right)^{2}+2(\sqrt{a}x^{2}-\sqrt{a}xx_{i})(\left\|{\bm{\beta}}\right\|-\sqrt{a}x^{2}+(\sqrt{a}x-\left\|{\bm{\alpha}}\right\|)x_{i})\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{2}\left(\left(\sqrt{a}x^{2}-\sqrt{a}xx_{i}\right)^{2}-\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{a}x^{2}}{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{a}x}{\varepsilon}x_{i}\right)\sqrt{a}x(x+x_{i})\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{2}ax^{2}\left(x-x_{i}\right)^{2}-\mathcal{O}\left(\left(1+x+\frac{1}{x}\right)\left(\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}(x_{i}^{2}+x_{i}^{3}+x_{i}^{4})\right){\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{2}ax^{2}\left(x-x_{i}\right)^{2}-\mathcal{O}\left(ax^{5}{\varepsilon}\right).

Rearranging terms above yields

\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}x_{i}^{2}\left(x-x_{i}\right)^{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\left((1+x^{2}+x^{3}){\varepsilon}\right)\leq\mathcal{O}\left(C_{x}^{3}{\varepsilon}\right).

∎

Appendix C
Local convergence

The goal of this section is to establish local convergence when the loss is below a certain threshold
{\varepsilon}_{0}
.

Below is the main result for local phase that shows we achieve
{\varepsilon}
loss within polynomial time.
See
4.7

The main contribution of this section is the landscape result presented in Theorem
C.1
, which provides a lower bound on the gradient norm. This result can then be combined with a standard descent lemma in Appendix
F
to establish convergence of the algorithm.

Theorem C.1

(Gradient norm lower bound)
.

Under Assumption
1
,
2
,
3
, given
{\bm{\mu}},\bm{\pi}
such that loss
{\varepsilon}:=\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}},\bm{\pi})\leq{\varepsilon}_{0}
with any
{\varepsilon}_{0}=\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))
,
U({\bm{\mu}})\leq B^{2}
and
\bm{\pi}
is near optimal in the sense that

\pi_{i}\left|\nabla_{\pi_{i}}\mathcal{L}+1\right|=\left|\mathbf{E}_{\bm{x}}\left[\frac{\pi_{i}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p({\bm{x}})}\right]-\pi_{i}\right|\leq{\varepsilon}_{\pi}\lesssim{\varepsilon}/n,

then we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\mu}}\mathcal{L}\right\|_{F}\gtrsim\frac{1}{\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})}\mathcal{L}^{3/4}.

We also have the regularity condition on
U
to show its remain bounded. That is, component
{\bm{\mu}}_{i}
will not move away from its nearest ground-truth.

Theorem C.2
.

Under Theorem
4.7
we have
U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\leq B^{2}
for all
t\geq T_{1}
.

Given above two results, we are able to prove Theorem
4.7
.

Proof of Theorem
4.7
.

By Theorem
C.2
we know
U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\leq B^{2}
for all
t\geq T_{1}
.
Then by Lemma
F.2
and Theorem
C.1
we have

\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})\leq\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})-\frac{\eta}{2}\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\mu}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})\right\|_{F}^{2}\leq\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})-\frac{\eta}{A}\mathcal{L}^{3/2}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)}),

where
A=\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
.

Note that the above inequality implies
\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})\leq\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})
, therefore (in the equation below, we omit the dependency on
\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)}
as all of them depends on it)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})}-\sqrt{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})-\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})}{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})(\sqrt{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})}+\sqrt{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})})}\geq\frac{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})-\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})}{2\mathcal{L}^{3/2}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})}\geq\frac{\eta}{2A}.

Thus,

\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t)})}}\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})}}\geq\frac{\eta}{2A}.

This leads to

\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})}}\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t)})}}+\frac{\eta}{2A}\geq\frac{\eta(t+1-T_{1})}{2A}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(T_{1})},\bm{\pi}^{(T_{1})})}}.

Rearranging the term gives the result.
∎

C.1
Gradient norm lower bound (Theorem
C.1
)

In this part, our goal is to provide a gradient norm lower bound, so that together with standard analysis in optimization we can show the algorithm decreases the loss after every step.

See
C.1

It follows from the following descent direction, as discussed in Section
4.3
.

Lemma C.3

(Descent direction)
.

Under Theorem
C.1
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\frac{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\geq\mathcal{L}/4,

where
\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}:=\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\pi_{i}
is the total weights belong to
S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})
that is
\delta_{\rm close}
-close to
{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}
(in
\left\|\cdot\right\|_{2}
sense), and
\delta_{\rm close}={\varepsilon}^{1/4}A
with
A=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{mnd}}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{9/4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3/2}D_{\max}^{5}\right)
as (
8
) in Theorem
B.1
.

Proof of Theorem
C.1
.

From Lemma
C.3
we know

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\frac{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\geq\mathcal{L}/4,

where
\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}:=\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\pi_{i}
is the total weights belong to
S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})
that is
\delta_{\rm close}
-close to
{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}
(in
\left\|\cdot\right\|_{2}
sense).

From (
8
) in Theorem
B.1
, we know we can choose
\delta_{\rm close}={\varepsilon}^{1/4}A
with
A=\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
such that
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\leq\delta_{\rm close}
and
\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}\gtrsim\pi_{\ell}^{*}
. Thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\frac{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\left(\frac{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}\right)^{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\cdot\left\|\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}}\mathcal{L}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\delta_{\rm close}\left\|\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}}\mathcal{L}\right\|_{F}\leq\sqrt{n}A{\varepsilon}^{1/4}\left\|\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}}\mathcal{L}\right\|_{F}.

Combining and rearranging above gives the desired bound.
∎

C.1.1
Proof of Lemma
C.3

The idea is to show the gradient
\nabla\mathcal{L}
has a nontrivial correlation with a constructed direction which we call it descent direction. Intuitively, such direction shows components close to one of the ground-truth
{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}
should move towards that.

See
C.3

Proof.

Recall
\hat{\pi}_{\ell}:=\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{i}
is the total weights belongs to
S_{\ell}
and
\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}:=\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\pi_{i}
is the total weights belong to
S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})
that is
\delta_{\rm close}
-close to
{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}
(in
\left\|\cdot\right\|_{2}
sense).

In the following, we consider

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\frac{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle+\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}(\nabla_{\pi_{i}}\mathcal{L}+1)\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})+\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}\setminus S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}(\nabla_{\pi_{i}}\mathcal{L}+1)\cdot\pi_{i}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\frac{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}}),{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\cdot\pi_{i}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\right]+\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}\setminus S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\cdot\pi_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\frac{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\Big[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\Big(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i}^{*};{\bm{x}})+\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\Big)\cdot\pi_{i}\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\right]+\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}\setminus S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\cdot\pi_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{c})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))(p({\bm{x}})-p^{*}({\bm{x}}))\right]+\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]}_{\hypertarget{termtaugd}{\text{(A)}}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{d})}}{{\geq}}

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}+\hyperlink{termtaugd}{\text{\text{(A)}}}

where
(a) recall
\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}:=\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\pi_{i}
so
\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})+\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}\setminus S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\pi_{i}=\sum_{\ell}(\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})+\sum_{\ell}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell})=0
;
(b) we use the expansion

\displaystyle\phi({\bm{\mu}}^{*};{\bm{x}})=\phi({\bm{\mu}};{\bm{x}})+\langle\nabla_{\bm{\mu}}\phi({\bm{\mu}};{\bm{x}}),{\bm{\mu}}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}\rangle+\phi({\bm{\mu}};{\bm{x}})\tau({\bm{x}}),

where
\tau({\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})=\phi({\bm{\mu}}^{*};{\bm{x}})/\phi({\bm{\mu}};{\bm{x}})-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\mu}}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}\rangle
. We will write
\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})=\tau({\bm{\mu}}_{i},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})
for
i\in S_{\ell}
;
(c) we rearrange the terms (recall
p({\bm{x}})=\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{i}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})
and similar for
p_{*}({\bm{x}})
), and especially use

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}\pi_{i}\right]+\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\cdot\pi_{i}\right],

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}\pi_{i}\right]=\sum_{\ell}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})\cdot\pi_{\ell}^{*}\right]=\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))p_{*}({\bm{x}})\right];

(d) loss
\ell(z)=-\log(z)
is convex so

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))(p({\bm{x}})-p^{*}({\bm{x}}))\right]\geq\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}[\ell(p({\bm{x}}))-\ell(p^{*}({\bm{x}}))]={\varepsilon}.

Since
\pi_{i}\left|\nabla_{\pi_{i}}\mathcal{L}+1\right|\leq{\varepsilon}_{\pi}\leq{\varepsilon}/4
, we know

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\frac{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\geq{\varepsilon}+\hyperlink{termtaugd}{\text{\text{(A)}}}-O(n){\varepsilon}_{\pi}\geq{\varepsilon}/2+\hyperlink{termtaugd}{\text{\text{(A)}}},

where we set
\delta_{\rm close}={\varepsilon}^{1/4}A
with
A=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{mnd}}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{9/4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3/2}D_{\max}^{5}\right)
so that
\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}\geq\pi_{\ell}^{*}/2
as (
8
) in Theorem
B.1

To deal with
(A)
, we will use the exact same argument as in the proof of Lemma
C.4
to deal with
(I)
. The only difference is that now the term is
\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}
while in
(I)
is
\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}
which has more terms to deal with. We therefore omit the details to avoid repetition. We can get
\hyperlink{termtaugd}{\text{\text{(A)}}}\geq-0.02{\varepsilon}
.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\frac{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\geq{\varepsilon}/2-0.02{\varepsilon}\geq{\varepsilon}/4=\mathcal{L}/4.

∎

C.2
Norm bound

In this part, we focus on the regularity condition that components
{\bm{\mu}}
will not move too far away in the local regime. Recall the potential

U({\bm{\mu}}):=\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

The following result relies on the fact that first order term of
U({\bm{\mu}})
changing is negative (Lemma
C.4
). Therefore, when stepsize
\eta
is small enough,
U
will not increase, showing components
{\bm{\mu}}
will not move too far away.
See
C.2

Proof.

First from Lemma
F.2
we know
\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t)})\leq\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(T_{1})},\bm{\pi}^{(T_{1})})\leq{\varepsilon}_{0}
.
By Lemma
C.4
we have

\displaystyle U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})

\displaystyle=U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})-\eta\sum_{j}\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\rangle+\eta^{2}\sum_{i\in[n]}\left\|\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})-\frac{\eta\mathcal{L}}{4}+\eta^{2}\sum_{i\in[n]}\left\|\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\right\|_{2}^{2}.

Note that

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[n]}\left\|\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\right\|_{2}^{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[n]}\left\|\sum_{j\in[m]}\pi_{j}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim j}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\sum_{k\in[n]}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*})\right]\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[n]}\left\|\sum_{j\in[m]}\pi_{j}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim j}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})\right]\right\|_{2}^{2}+D_{\max}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})+D_{\max}^{2}

Thus, when
\eta\lesssim{\varepsilon}/D_{\max}^{2}
we know

\displaystyle U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})\leq U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\leq B^{2}.

∎

The key in the above proof is the following gradient projection bound. This is similar to the descent direction in Lemma
C.3
.

Lemma C.4
.

Under Assumption
1
,
2
,
3
,
given
{\bm{\mu}},\bm{\pi}
such that loss
{\varepsilon}:=\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}},\bm{\pi})\leq{\varepsilon}_{0}
with any
{\varepsilon}_{0}=\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))
,
\bm{\pi}
is near optimal in the sense that

\pi_{i}\left|\nabla_{\pi_{i}}\mathcal{L}+1\right|=\left|\mathbf{E}_{\bm{x}}\left[\frac{\pi_{i}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p({\bm{x}})}\right]-\pi_{i}\right|\leq{\varepsilon}_{\pi}\leq\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}/\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max}),

U({\bm{\mu}})\leq B^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\geq\mathcal{L}/4.

Proof.

In the following, we consider

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle+\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}(\nabla_{\pi_{i}}\mathcal{L}+1)\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}}),{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\cdot\pi_{i}\right]+\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\Big[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\Big(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i}^{*};{\bm{x}})+\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\Big)\cdot\pi_{i}\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{c})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))(p({\bm{x}})-p^{*}({\bm{x}}))\right]+\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]}_{\hypertarget{termtau}{\text{(I)}}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}}))\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\right]}_{\hypertarget{termII}{\text{(II)}}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{d})}}{{\geq}}

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}+\hyperlink{termtau}{\text{\text{(I)}}}+\hyperlink{termII}{\text{\text{(II)}}}

where
(a) denote
\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}:=\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\pi_{i}
so
\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})=\sum_{\ell}\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}=0
;
(b) we use the expansion

\displaystyle\phi({\bm{\mu}}^{*};{\bm{x}})=\phi({\bm{\mu}};{\bm{x}})+\langle\nabla_{\bm{\mu}}\phi({\bm{\mu}};{\bm{x}}),{\bm{\mu}}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}\rangle+\phi({\bm{\mu}};{\bm{x}})\tau({\bm{x}}),

where
\tau({\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})=\phi({\bm{\mu}}^{*};{\bm{x}})/\phi({\bm{\mu}};{\bm{x}})-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}},{\bm{\mu}}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}\rangle
. We will write
\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})=\tau({\bm{\mu}}_{i},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})
for
i\in S_{\ell}
;
(c) we rearrange the terms (recall
p({\bm{x}})=\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{i}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})
and similar for
p_{*}({\bm{x}})
), and especially use

\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\right]=\sum_{\ell}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})\cdot(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\right];

(d) loss
\ell(z)=-\log(z)
is convex so

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))(p({\bm{x}})-p^{*}({\bm{x}}))\right]\geq\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}[\ell(p({\bm{x}}))-\ell(p^{*}({\bm{x}}))]={\varepsilon}.

Since
\pi_{i}\left|\nabla_{\pi_{i}}\mathcal{L}+1\right|\leq{\varepsilon}_{\pi}\leq\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}/\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
, we know

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\geq{\varepsilon}+\hyperlink{termtau}{\text{\text{(I)}}}+\hyperlink{termII}{\text{\text{(II)}}}-{\varepsilon}_{\pi}\sum_{\ell}\frac{|\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}|}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}\geq{\varepsilon}/2+\hyperlink{termtau}{\text{\text{(I)}}}+\hyperlink{termII}{\text{\text{(II)}}},

where we use
|\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}|\leq\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
from (
7
) in Theorem
B.1
and set
\delta_{\rm close}={\varepsilon}^{1/4}A
with
A=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{mnd}}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{9/4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3/2}D_{\max}^{5}\right)
so that
\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}\geq\pi_{\ell}^{*}/2
as (
8
) in Theorem
B.1
.

In the following, the goal is to deal with
(I)
and
(II)
respectively.

From Lemma
C.5
and Lemma
D.10

we know for some constant
C

\displaystyle\hyperlink{termtau}{\text{\text{(I)}}}\geq-0.02{\varepsilon},\quad|\hyperlink{termII}{\text{\text{(II)}}}|\lesssim{\varepsilon}^{9/8}.

Since
{\varepsilon}\leq{\varepsilon}_{0}
is small enough, we can have

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\langle\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\geq{\varepsilon}/2+\hyperlink{termtau}{\text{\text{(I)}}}+\hyperlink{termII}{\text{\text{(II)}}}\geq{\varepsilon}/4=\mathcal{L}/4\geq 0,

∎

C.2.1
Bound
(I)

In this part, we bound
(I)
. We will further split it into more terms to deal with later. Bound
(II)
is deferred to Appendix
D.4
.

High-level roadmap

We sketch a rough proof plan of how to bound
(I)
here, aiming to show the high-level idea behind the seemingly heavy calculation in Section
D
. The calculation here is only for the purpose of illustrating and is not rigorous.

We first expand
\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))
at
p_{*}({\bm{x}})
.

\displaystyle\hyperlink{termtau}{\text{\text{(I)}}}:=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p_{*}({\bm{x}}))\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]}_{(I.i)}+\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime\prime}(p_{*}({\bm{x}}))R({\bm{x}})\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]}_{(I.ii)},

where the remain high-order term will be handled in Lemma
D.1
.

Then, note
\ell^{\prime}(u)=-1/u

\displaystyle(I.i)\approx\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p_{*}({\bm{x}}))\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]=\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\int_{\bm{x}}-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}{\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}=0

where we use Lemma
C.6
in the last equation. See formal result in Lemma
D.2
.

Also, note that
\ell^{\prime\prime}(u)=1/u^{2}

\displaystyle(I.ii)\approx\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime\prime}(p_{*}({\bm{x}}))R({\bm{x}})\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]=\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\int_{{\bm{x}}}\frac{R({\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}{\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}},

where the remain high-order term are handled in Lemma
D.5
and Lemma
D.4
.

We then further introduce the expansion of
\phi
as:

\displaystyle\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})=\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})+\langle\nabla_{\bm{\mu}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}}),{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle+\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}}),

\displaystyle\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})=\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})+\langle\nabla_{\bm{\mu}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}}),{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle+\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})\widetilde{\tau}_{i}({\bm{x}}).

This leads to

\displaystyle R({\bm{x}})=\sum_{j}(\hat{\pi}_{j}-\pi_{j}^{*})\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})+\sum_{j}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\pi_{k}{\bm{\mu}}_{k}-\hat{\pi}_{j}{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}\rangle+\sum_{j}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\pi_{k}\widetilde{\tau}_{k}({\bm{x}}),

so we will further split (I.ii) into 3 terms. Each of terms will be handled in Lemma
D.7
, Lemma
D.8
and Lemma
D.9
. The proof relies on the property of
\tau,\widetilde{\tau}
in Lemma
C.6
.

We now present the formal result of bounding
(I)
.

Lemma C.5
.

Under Lemma
C.4
, we have with some constant
C_{1}

\displaystyle\hyperlink{termtau}{\text{\text{(I)}}}:=\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\geq-0.02{\varepsilon}

Proof.

We are going to expand
\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))
at position
p_{\theta}({\bm{x}}):=\theta p({\bm{x}})+(1-\theta)p^{*}({\bm{x}})
with some
\theta\in(0,1)
that will be chosen later. Note that
p(x)-p_{\theta}(x)=(1-\theta)(p({\bm{x}})-p^{*}({\bm{x}}))=(1-\theta)R({\bm{x}})
, where
R({\bm{x}}):=p({\bm{x}})-p^{*}({\bm{x}})
is the residual. We have

\displaystyle\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))=\ell^{\prime}(p_{\theta}({\bm{x}}))+\ell^{\prime\prime}(p_{\theta}({\bm{x}}))\cdot(1-\theta)R({\bm{x}})+\ell^{\prime\prime\prime}\left((1-t({\bm{x}}))p({\bm{x}})+t({\bm{x}})p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})\right)\cdot(1-\theta)^{2}R^{2}({\bm{x}}),

where
t({\bm{x}})\in[0,1]
.

Thus, we further expand
(I)
into following terms:

\displaystyle\hyperlink{termtau}{\text{\text{(I)}}}=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p_{\theta}({\bm{x}}))\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]}_{\hypertarget{termtau1st}{\text{(I.i)}}}+(1-\theta)\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime\prime}(p_{\theta}({\bm{x}}))R({\bm{x}})\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]}_{\hypertarget{termtau2nd}{\text{(I.ii)}}}

\displaystyle+(1-\theta)^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime\prime\prime}\left((1-t({\bm{x}}))p({\bm{x}})+t({\bm{x}})p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})\right)R^{2}({\bm{x}})\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]}_{\hypertarget{termtau3rd}{\text{(I.iii)}}}.

From Lemma
D.2
, Lemma
D.3
and Lemma
D.1
we know for some constant
C
and choose
\theta
such that
{\varepsilon}^{1/28}\lesssim\theta\leq C^{-1}\exp(-CD_{\max}B)/\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
with large enough constant
C
, then

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termtau1st}{\text{\text{(I.i)}}}|\leq 0.01{\varepsilon},\quad\hyperlink{termtau2nd}{\text{\text{(I.ii)}}}\gtrsim-\exp\left(-\Theta(\Delta^{2})\right){\varepsilon},\quad|\hyperlink{termtau3rd}{\text{\text{(I.iii)}}}|\lesssim{\varepsilon}^{13/12}.

This implies
\hyperlink{termtau}{\text{\text{(I)}}}\geq-0.02{\varepsilon}
.
∎

C.2.2
Technical lemma

The following lemma collects few useful properties of
\tau_{i}
in the proof. Recall its definition for
i\in S_{\ell}
:

\displaystyle\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})=\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})+\langle\nabla_{\bm{\mu}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}}),{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle+\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}}),

\displaystyle\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})=\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})+\langle\nabla_{\bm{\mu}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}}),{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle+\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})\widetilde{\tau}_{i}({\bm{x}}).

We can see that
\tau
and
\widetilde{\tau}
can be viewed as the 2nd order remainder term in the Taylor expansion.

Lemma C.6

(Property of
\tau_{i}
and
\widetilde{\tau}_{i}
)
.

For
i\in S_{\ell}
and constant
\alpha\geq 1
, if
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\leq B\leq D_{\max}
, then there exists constant
C
(depending only on
\alpha
) such that

\displaystyle\left\|\tau_{i}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\leq

\displaystyle C\exp(CD_{\max}B)\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\tau}_{i}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\leq

\displaystyle C\exp(CD_{\max}B)\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2},

where
\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})=\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})/\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle
and
\widetilde{\tau}_{i}({\bm{x}})=\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})/\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle
. Also, we have for
i\in S_{\ell}

\displaystyle\int_{\bm{x}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}}){\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}=0,\quad\int_{\bm{x}}\left({\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right)\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})=0,

and for
i,k\in S_{\ell}

\displaystyle\int_{\bm{x}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\widetilde{\tau}_{k}({\bm{x}}){\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}\geq 0.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\left\|\tau_{i}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})/\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle\right)^{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{\bm{x}}\left[\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle}-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle\right)^{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\pi_{j}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim j}\left[\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle+\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle}-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle-\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle\right)^{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\pi_{j}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim j}\Big[\Big(e^{-\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle+\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle}-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle-\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Big)^{\alpha}\Big]+\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(D_{\max}^{2\alpha}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2\alpha}+\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{4\alpha})\exp{(O(D_{\max}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|))},

where we use Lemma
D.17
in the last line.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\tau}_{i}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}^{\alpha}=

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})/\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\right)^{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{\bm{x}}\left[\left(e^{-\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle}-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\right)^{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\pi_{j}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim j}\left[\left(e^{-\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle}-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle-\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\right)^{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\pi_{j}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim j}\Big[\Big(e^{-\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle+\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle}-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle-\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Big)^{\alpha}\Big]+\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(D_{\max}^{2\alpha}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2\alpha}+\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{4\alpha})\exp{(O(D_{\max}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|))},

where we use Lemma
D.17
in the last line.

The first part of second property follows direction from the definition of
\tau_{i}
. To see this,

\displaystyle\int_{\bm{x}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}}){\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}=

\displaystyle\int_{\bm{x}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle{\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}=0

\displaystyle\int_{\bm{x}}\left({\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right)\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})=

\displaystyle\int_{\bm{x}}\left({\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right)\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})-\left({\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right)\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\left({\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right)\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle{\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0-({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})-({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i})=0.

For the second part of second property, note that we can also write
\tau_{i}
and
\widetilde{\tau}_{i}
as sum of Hermite polynomials using generating function (
5
)

\displaystyle\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})=

\displaystyle\exp\left(\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle=\sum_{j\geq 2}\frac{1}{j!}\langle\He_{j}({\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}),({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i})^{\otimes j}\rangle,

\displaystyle\widetilde{\tau}_{i}({\bm{x}})=

\displaystyle\exp\left(\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle=\sum_{j\geq 2}\frac{1}{j!}\langle\He_{j}({\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}),({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})^{\otimes j}\rangle,

where we use
\He_{0}({\bm{x}})=1
and
\He_{1}({\bm{x}})={\bm{x}}
.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\bm{x}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\widetilde{\tau}_{k}({\bm{x}}){\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}=

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim N({\bm{\mu}}_{i},{\bm{I}})}\left[\sum_{j\geq 2}\frac{1}{j!}\langle\He_{j}({\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}),({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i})^{\otimes j}\rangle\cdot\sum_{j\geq 2}\frac{1}{j!}\langle\He_{j}({\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}),({\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})^{\otimes j}\rangle\right]

It suffices to prove the following claim (shift everything by
{\bm{\mu}}_{i}
):

Claim C.1
.

For any
{\bm{v}},{\bm{u}},{\bm{w}}
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim N(0,{\bm{I}})}\left[\sum_{j\geq 2}\frac{1}{j!}\langle\He_{j}({\bm{x}}),{\bm{v}}^{\otimes j}\rangle\cdot\sum_{j\geq 2}\frac{1}{j!}\langle\He_{j}({\bm{x}}+{\bm{u}}),{\bm{w}}^{\otimes j}\rangle\right]\geq 0

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim N(0,{\bm{I}})}\left[\sum_{j\geq 2}\frac{1}{j!}\langle\He_{j}({\bm{x}}),{\bm{v}}^{\otimes j}\rangle\cdot\sum_{j\geq 2}\frac{1}{j!}\langle\He_{j}({\bm{x}}+{\bm{u}}),{\bm{w}}^{\otimes j}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j,s\geq 2}\frac{1}{j!s!}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim N(0,{\bm{I}})}\left[\langle\He_{j}({\bm{x}}),{\bm{v}}^{\otimes j}\rangle\cdot\langle\He_{s}({\bm{x}}+{\bm{u}}),{\bm{w}}^{\otimes s}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\sum_{s\geq 2}\sum_{2\leq j\leq s}\frac{1}{s!}\binom{s}{j}\langle{\bm{u}}^{\otimes(s-j)},{\bm{w}}^{\otimes(s-j)}\rangle\langle{\bm{v}}^{\otimes j},{\bm{w}}^{\otimes j}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s\geq 2}\frac{1}{s!}\left(\langle{\bm{u}}+{\bm{v}},{\bm{w}}\rangle^{s}-\langle{\bm{u}},{\bm{w}}\rangle^{s}-s\langle{\bm{v}},{\bm{w}}\rangle\langle{\bm{u}},{\bm{w}}\rangle^{s-1}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\exp\left(\langle{\bm{u}}+{\bm{v}},{\bm{w}}\rangle\right)-1-\langle{\bm{u}}+{\bm{v}},{\bm{w}}\rangle\right)-\left(\exp\left(\langle{\bm{u}},{\bm{w}}\rangle\right)-1-\langle{\bm{u}},{\bm{w}}\rangle\right)-\langle{\bm{v}},{\bm{w}}\rangle\left(\exp\left(\langle{\bm{u}},{\bm{w}}\rangle\right)-1\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\exp(\langle{\bm{u}},{\bm{w}}\rangle)\left(\exp(\langle{\bm{v}},{\bm{w}}\rangle)-1-\langle{\bm{v}},{\bm{w}}\rangle\right)\geq 0,

where (a) we use Lemma
A.4
.
∎

Thus, from the above claim we know

\int_{\bm{x}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\widetilde{\tau}_{k}({\bm{x}}){\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}\geq 0.

∎

This lemma connects the loss and chi-square like form, which is useful in the analysis.

Claim C.2
.

Denote
p_{\theta}=\theta p+(1-\theta)p_{*}
. There exists a constant
c
such that for
0<\theta<c

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(1-\theta)\log\left(\frac{1}{1-\theta}\right)}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\pi},{\bm{\mu}})\leq\frac{2}{\theta}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\pi},{\bm{\mu}}),

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(1-\theta)^{2}\log\left(\frac{1}{1-\theta}\right)}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\pi},{\bm{\mu}})\leq\frac{2}{\theta}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\pi},{\bm{\mu}}),

\displaystyle\left|\frac{R({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\theta}.

Proof.

From Corollary
B.3
, we know

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\bm{\pi},{\bm{\mu}})=D_{\text{KL}}(p_{*}||p)\geq\frac{1-\theta}{\theta^{2}}\log\left(\frac{1}{1-\theta}\right)\chi^{2}(p_{*}||p_{\theta})=(1-\theta)\log\left(\frac{1}{1-\theta}\right)\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right],

where
R({\bm{x}})=p({\bm{x}})-p_{*}({\bm{x}})=\frac{1}{\theta}(p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})-p_{*}({\bm{x}}))
.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right]=\theta\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p}\left[\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right]+(1-\theta)\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right]\geq(1-\theta)\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right].

Also,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{R({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\right|\leq\frac{1}{\theta}+\frac{1}{1-\theta}\leq\frac{2}{\theta}.

∎

Appendix D
Omitted proofs in Appendix
C.2

In this section we give the detailed proof of bound
(I)
and
(II)
. Recall
\hyperlink{termtau}{\text{\text{(I)}}}=\hyperlink{termtau1st}{\text{\text{(I.i)}}}+\hyperlink{termtau2nd}{\text{\text{(I.ii)}}}+\hyperlink{termtau3rd}{\text{\text{(I.iii)}}}
, each of these will be handled in Section
D.1
(bound
(I.iii)
), Section
D.2
(bound
(I.i)
) and Section
D.3
(bound
(I.ii)
).

(II)
will be handled in Section
D.4
.

D.1
Bound (I.iii)

The following lemma gives the bound on the 3rd order term
(I.iii)
.

Lemma D.1
.

Under Lemma
C.4
, for
1\gtrsim\theta\gtrsim{\varepsilon}^{1/28}
we have

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termtau3rd}{\text{\text{(I.iii)}}}|:=\left|\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime\prime\prime}\left((1-t({\bm{x}}))p({\bm{x}})+t({\bm{x}})p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})\right)R^{2}({\bm{x}})\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\right|\lesssim{\varepsilon}^{13/12}

Proof.

Note that
p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})/p({\bm{x}})\geq\theta
. Then recall
\ell^{\prime}(u)=-1/u
, by Lemma
D.16
we know
t({\bm{x}})\geq\frac{2}{3}\theta^{1/3}\left(\frac{p({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{1/3}
and
t({\bm{x}})\in[0,1]
. Using
\ell^{\prime\prime\prime}(u)=-2/u^{3}
, we know

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termtau3rd}{\text{\text{(I.iii)}}}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\frac{1}{\left((1-t({\bm{x}}))p({\bm{x}})+t({\bm{x}})p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})\right)^{3}}R^{2}({\bm{x}})\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})|\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})|\pi_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\frac{1}{\left(t({\bm{x}})p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})\right)^{3}}R^{2}({\bm{x}})\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})|\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})|\pi_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\frac{1}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})p({\bm{x}})}\frac{1}{\theta}R^{2}({\bm{x}})\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})|\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})|\pi_{i}\right]

where (a) we use
t({\bm{x}})\in[0,1]
,
p({\bm{x}}),p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})\geq 0
; (b) we use
t({\bm{x}})\geq\frac{2}{3}\theta^{1/3}\left(\frac{p({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{1/3}
.

For each term above, we have for constant
\alpha>1
(choose later)

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\frac{1}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})p({\bm{x}})}\frac{1}{\theta}R^{2}({\bm{x}})\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})|\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})|\pi_{i}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\theta}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\left(\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]^{1/\alpha}\mathbf{E}_{\bm{x}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p({\bm{x}})}|\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})|\pi_{i}\right)^{1/(1-1/\alpha)}\right]^{1-1/\alpha}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\theta}{\varepsilon}^{1/\alpha}\frac{1}{\theta^{(2\alpha-1)/\alpha}}\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p}\right\|_{2/(1-1/\alpha)}\pi_{i}\left\|\tau_{i}\right\|_{2/(1-1/\alpha)}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}^{1/\alpha}\frac{1}{\theta^{3-1/\alpha}}\cdot\frac{n}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}e^{\mathcal{O}\left(D_{\max}B\right)}\cdot\pi_{i}e^{\mathcal{O}\left(D_{\max}B\right)}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{ne^{\mathcal{O}\left(D_{\max}B\right)}}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}\theta^{3-1/\alpha}}{\varepsilon}^{1/\alpha}\cdot\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

where
(a) we have
\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\leq\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right)\left(\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha-1}\right]\lesssim\frac{{\varepsilon}}{\theta}\frac{1}{\theta^{2\alpha-2}}
by Claim
C.2
, and
\left\|fg\right\|_{\beta}\leq\left\|f\right\|_{2\beta}\left\|g\right\|_{2\beta}
;
(b) we use
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\leq B
, Lemma
D.14
and Lemma
C.6
.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termtau3rd}{\text{\text{(I.iii)}}}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{ne^{\mathcal{O}\left(D_{\max}B\right)}}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}\theta^{3-1/\alpha}}{\varepsilon}^{1/\alpha}\cdot\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\frac{\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})e^{\mathcal{O}\left(D_{\max}B\right)}}{\theta^{3-1/\alpha}}{\varepsilon}^{1/\alpha+1/2},

where (a) we use (
6
) in Theorem
B.1
.

Taking
\alpha<2
, say
\alpha=3/2
, and as long as
\theta>O({\varepsilon}^{1/28})
and
{\varepsilon}\leq{\varepsilon}_{0}=\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))
, we have
|\hyperlink{termtau3rd}{\text{\text{(I.iii)}}}|\lesssim{\varepsilon}^{13/12}
.
∎

D.2
Bound (I.i)

The following lemma gives the bound on the 1st order term
(I.i)
.

Lemma D.2
.

Under Lemma
C.4
, for
0<\theta\leq C^{-1}\exp(-CD_{\max}B)/\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
with large enough constant
C
we have

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termtau1st}{\text{\text{(I.i)}}}|:=\left|\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p_{\theta}({\bm{x}}))\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\right|\leq 0.01{\varepsilon}

Proof.

Note that
\ell^{\prime}(u)=-1/u
. We have

\displaystyle\hyperlink{termtau1st}{\text{\text{(I.i)}}}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\int_{\bm{x}}\frac{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}{\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\int_{\bm{x}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}{\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}+\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\int_{\bm{x}}\frac{\theta R({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}{\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\int_{\bm{x}}\frac{\theta R({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}{\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}

where (a) we use
\frac{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}=1-\frac{\theta R({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}
; (b) we use
\int_{\bm{x}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}}){\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}=0
from Lemma
C.6
.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termtau1st}{\text{\text{(I.i)}}}|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\theta|R({\bm{x}})|}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}|\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})|\pi_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\theta\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right]\right)^{1/2}\cdot\pi_{i}\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi^{2}({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}^{2}({\bm{x}})\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\leq}}
