\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\theta\sqrt{\frac{2{\varepsilon}}{\theta}}\cdot\pi_{i}\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{4}\left\|\tau_{i}\right\|_{4}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\sqrt{{\varepsilon}\theta}\cdot\frac{1}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\exp(\mathcal{O}\left(D_{\max}B\right))\cdot\pi_{i}\exp(\mathcal{O}\left(D_{\max}B\right))\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{{\varepsilon}\theta}\cdot\frac{1}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\exp(\mathcal{O}\left(D_{\max}B\right))\cdot\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{c})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\sqrt{\theta}\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})\exp(\mathcal{O}\left(D_{\max}B\right))\cdot{\varepsilon}

where (a) we use Claim
C.2
; (b) we use Lemma
D.14
and Lemma
C.6
; (c) we use (
6
) in Theorem
B.1
.

As long as
\theta\leq C^{-1}\exp(-CD_{\max}B)/\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
with large enough constant
C
, we know

|\hyperlink{termtau1st}{\text{\text{(I.i)}}}|\leq 0.01{\varepsilon}.

∎

D.3
Bound (I.ii)

The following lemma gives the bound on the 2nd order term
(I.ii)
. In fact, we will split
(I.ii)
into (I.ii.1)+(I.ii.2)+(I.ii.3). Each of these will be handled in Section
D.3.3
(bound (I.ii.1)), Section
D.3.2
(bound (I.ii.2)) and Section
D.3.1
(bound (I.ii.3)).

Lemma D.3
.

Under Lemma
C.4
, for
1\gtrsim\theta\gtrsim{\varepsilon}^{1/28}
, then we have

\displaystyle\hyperlink{termtau2nd}{\text{\text{(I.ii)}}}:=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime\prime}(p_{\theta}({\bm{x}}))R({\bm{x}})\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\gtrsim-\exp\left(-\Theta(\Delta^{2})\right){\varepsilon}.

Proof.

Note that
\ell^{\prime\prime}(u)=1/u^{2}
. We have

\displaystyle\hyperlink{termtau2nd}{\text{\text{(I.ii)}}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\int_{\bm{x}}\frac{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}R({\bm{x}})\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}{\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\int_{\bm{x}}\frac{1}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}R({\bm{x}})\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}{\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}-2\theta\int_{\bm{x}}\frac{R({\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}R({\bm{x}})\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}{\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\theta^{2}\int_{\bm{x}}\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}R({\bm{x}})\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}{\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{R({\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]}_{\hypertarget{termtau2nd1}{\text{(I.ii.1)}}}-2\theta\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]}_{\hypertarget{termtau2nd2}{\text{(I.ii.2)}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\theta^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\frac{R^{3}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{3}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]}_{\hypertarget{termtau2nd3}{\text{(I.ii.3)}}}

where (a) we use
\frac{p_{*}}{p_{\theta}^{2}}=\frac{1}{p_{*}}\frac{p_{*}^{2}}{p_{\theta}^{2}}=\frac{1}{p_{*}}\left(1-\frac{\theta R}{p_{\theta}}\right)^{2}=\frac{1}{p_{*}}-2\theta\frac{R}{p_{*}p_{\theta}}+\theta^{2}\frac{R^{2}}{p_{*}p_{\theta}^{2}}
.

From Lemma
D.6
, Lemma
D.5
and Lemma
D.4
we know

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termtau2nd2}{\text{\text{(I.ii.2)}}}|,|\hyperlink{termtau2nd3}{\text{\text{(I.ii.3)}}}|\lesssim{\varepsilon}^{13/12},\quad\hyperlink{termtau2nd1}{\text{\text{(I.ii.1)}}}\gtrsim-\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/36\right){\varepsilon}.

This implies
\hyperlink{termtau2nd}{\text{\text{(I.ii)}}}\gtrsim-\exp\left(-\Theta(\Delta^{2})\right){\varepsilon}
.
∎

D.3.1
Bound (I.ii.3)

Lemma D.4
.

Under Lemma
C.4
, for
1\gtrsim\theta\gtrsim{\varepsilon}^{1/28}
we have

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termtau2nd3}{\text{\text{(I.ii.3)}}}|:=

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\frac{R^{3}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{3}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\right|\lesssim{\varepsilon}^{13/12}.

Proof.

We have for constant
\alpha>1
that will be chosen later

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termtau2nd3}{\text{\text{(I.ii.3)}}}|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\left(\frac{R^{3}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{3}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}\cdot\pi_{i}\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\right)^{\frac{1}{1-1/\alpha}}\right]\right)^{1-1/\alpha}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}^{1/\alpha}\frac{1}{\theta^{3-1/\alpha}}\cdot\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{i}\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\frac{2}{1-1/\alpha}}\right]\right)^{\frac{1-1/\alpha}{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})^{\frac{2}{1-1/\alpha}}\right]\right)^{\frac{1-1/\alpha}{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}^{1/\alpha}\frac{1}{\theta^{3-1/\alpha}}\cdot\frac{1}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\exp(O(D_{\max}B))\cdot\exp(O(D_{\max}B))\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{c})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\frac{\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})}{\theta^{3-1/\alpha}}\exp(O(D_{\max}B)){\varepsilon}^{1/2+1/\alpha}

where (a) we use
\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\left(\frac{R^{3}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{3}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]=\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\left(\frac{R({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{3\alpha-2}\right]\lesssim\frac{{\varepsilon}}{\theta}\left(\frac{1}{\theta}\right)^{3\alpha-2}
; (b) we use Lemma
D.14
and Lemma
C.6
; (c) we use (
6
) in Theorem
B.1
.

Taking
\alpha<2
, say
\alpha=3/2
, and as long as
\theta>O({\varepsilon}^{1/28})
and
{\varepsilon}\leq{\varepsilon}_{0}=\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))
, we have
|\hyperlink{termtau2nd3}{\text{\text{(I.ii.3)}}}|\lesssim{\varepsilon}^{13/12}
.
∎

D.3.2
Bound (I.ii.2)

Lemma D.5
.

Under Lemma
C.4
, for
1\gtrsim\theta\gtrsim{\varepsilon}^{1/16}
we have

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termtau2nd2}{\text{\text{(I.ii.2)}}}|:=

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\right|\lesssim{\varepsilon}^{13/12}

Proof.

We have for constant
\alpha>1
that will be chosen later

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termtau2nd2}{\text{\text{(I.ii.2)}}}|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\left(\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}\cdot\pi_{i}\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\right)^{\frac{1}{1-1/\alpha}}\right]\right)^{1-1/\alpha}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}^{1/\alpha}\frac{1}{\theta^{2-1/\alpha}}\cdot\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{i}\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\frac{2}{1-1/\alpha}}\right]\right)^{\frac{1-1/\alpha}{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})^{\frac{2}{1-1/\alpha}}\right]\right)^{\frac{1-1/\alpha}{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}^{1/\alpha}\frac{1}{\theta^{2-1/\alpha}}\cdot\frac{1}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\exp(O(D_{\max}B))\cdot\exp(O(D_{\max}B))\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{c})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\frac{A}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}\theta^{2-1/\alpha}}\exp(O(D_{\max}B)){\varepsilon}^{1/2+1/\alpha}

where (a) we use
\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\left(\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]=\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\left(\frac{R({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{2\alpha-2}\right]\lesssim\frac{{\varepsilon}}{\theta}\left(\frac{1}{\theta}\right)^{2\alpha-2}
;
(b) we use Lemma
D.14
and Lemma
C.6
;
(c) we use (
6
) in Theorem
B.1
and
A=\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
is the coefficient in front of
\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}
.

Taking
\alpha<2
, say
\alpha=3/2
, and as long as
\theta>O({\varepsilon}^{1/16})
and
{\varepsilon}\leq{\varepsilon}_{0}=\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))
, we have
|\hyperlink{termtau2nd2}{\text{\text{(I.ii.2)}}}|\lesssim{\varepsilon}^{13/12}
.
∎

D.3.3
Bound (I.ii.1)

In this part, we give the bound of (I.ii.i). We will split it into 3 terms that will be handled in Lemma
D.7
, Lemma
D.8
and Lemma
D.9
.

Lemma D.6
.

Under Lemma
C.4
, then we have

\displaystyle\hyperlink{termtau2nd1}{\text{\text{(I.ii.1)}}}:=\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{R({\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\gtrsim-\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/36\right){\varepsilon}

Proof.

We first note that for
i\in S_{\ell}
we have the following Taylor expansion:

\displaystyle\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})=\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})+\langle\nabla_{\bm{\mu}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}}),{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle+\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})\widetilde{\tau}_{i}({\bm{x}}),

where
\widetilde{\tau}_{i}({\bm{x}}):=\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})/\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})-1-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle
. This gives

\displaystyle R({\bm{x}})=p({\bm{x}})-p_{*}({\bm{x}})

\displaystyle=\sum_{j}(\hat{\pi}_{j}-\pi_{j}^{*})\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})+\sum_{j}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\pi_{k}{\bm{\mu}}_{k}-\hat{\pi}_{j}{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}\rangle+\sum_{j}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\pi_{k}\widetilde{\tau}_{k}({\bm{x}}),

where recall
\hat{\pi}_{j}=\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\pi_{k}
.

Hence,

\displaystyle\hyperlink{termtau2nd1}{\text{\text{(I.ii.1)}}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{R({\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\sum_{j}(\hat{\pi}_{j}-\pi_{j}^{*})\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]}_{\hypertarget{termtau2nd11}{\text{(I.ii.1.1)}}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\sum_{j}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\pi_{k}{\bm{\mu}}_{k}-\hat{\pi}_{j}{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}\rangle\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]}_{\hypertarget{termtau2nd12}{\text{(I.ii.1.2)}}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\sum_{j}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\pi_{k}\widetilde{\tau}_{k}({\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]}_{\hypertarget{termtau2nd13}{\text{(I.ii.1.3)}}}.

From Lemma
D.7
, Lemma
D.8
and Lemma
D.9
, we know

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termtau2nd11}{\text{\text{(I.ii.1.1)}}}|,|\hyperlink{termtau2nd12}{\text{\text{(I.ii.1.2)}}}|\lesssim\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/36\right){\varepsilon},\quad\hyperlink{termtau2nd13}{\text{\text{(I.ii.1.3)}}}\gtrsim-\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/36\right){\varepsilon}.

This implies
\hyperlink{termtau2nd}{\text{\text{(I.ii)}}}\gtrsim-\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/36\right){\varepsilon}
.
∎

Lemma D.7
.

Under Lemma
C.4
, then we have

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termtau2nd11}{\text{\text{(I.ii.1.1)}}}|:=\left|\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\sum_{j}(\hat{\pi}_{j}-\pi_{j}^{*})\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\right|\lesssim\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/36\right){\varepsilon}

Proof.

We focus on each term in
(I.ii.1.1)
indexed by
\ell,i,j
in the sum.

If
\ell\neq j
, that is
i\not\in S_{j}

\displaystyle\left|(\hat{\pi}_{j}-\pi_{j}^{*})\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\right|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}A\cdot\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*})}{p_{*}}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{2,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\cdot\pi_{i}\left\|\tau_{i}\right\|_{2,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}A\cdot\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/34\right)\cdot\pi_{i}\exp(CD_{\max}B)\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/35\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\cdot\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2},

where (a) we use (
7
) in Theorem
B.1
and
A=\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
is the coefficient in front of
\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}
;
(b) we use Lemma
D.15
and Lemma
C.6
.

If
\ell=j
, that is
i\in S_{j}

\displaystyle(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]=

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(1-\sum_{k\neq\ell}\frac{\pi_{k}^{*}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{k}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\right)\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\sum_{k\neq\ell}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\pi_{k}^{*}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{k}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]

where (a) we use
\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\right]=\int_{\bm{x}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}}){\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}=0
from Lemma
C.6
. Thus,

\displaystyle\left|(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\right|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}A}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\cdot\sum_{k\neq\ell}\pi_{k}^{*}\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{k}^{*})}{p_{*}}\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{2,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\cdot\pi_{i}\left\|\tau_{i}\right\|_{2,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}A}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\cdot\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/34\right)\cdot\pi_{i}\exp(CD_{\max}B)\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/35\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\cdot\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

where (a) we use (
7
) in Theorem
B.1
and
A=\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
is the coefficient in front of
\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}
;
(b) we use Lemma
D.15
and Lemma
C.6
.

Combine above results with (
6
) in Theorem
B.1
that
\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
, we know
|\hyperlink{termtau2nd11}{\text{\text{(I.ii.1.1)}}}|\lesssim\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/36\right){\varepsilon}
.
∎

Lemma D.8
.

Under Lemma
C.4
, then we have

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termtau2nd12}{\text{\text{(I.ii.1.2)}}}|:=\left|\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\sum_{j}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\pi_{k}{\bm{\mu}}_{k}-\hat{\pi}_{j}{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}\rangle\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/36\right){\varepsilon}.

Proof.

We focus on each term in
(I.ii.1.2)
indexed by
\ell,i,j
in the sum.

If
\ell\neq j
, that is
i\not\in S_{j}

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\pi_{k}{\bm{\mu}}_{k}-\hat{\pi}_{j}{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}\rangle\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*})}{p_{*}}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{2,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\cdot\left\|\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\pi_{k}{\bm{\mu}}_{k}-\hat{\pi}_{j}{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}\right\|_{2}\cdot\pi_{i}\left\|\left\|{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}\right\|_{2}\tau_{i}\right\|_{2,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/34\right)\cdot\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}A\cdot\pi_{i}\poly(d)D_{\max}\exp(CD_{\max}B)\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/35\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\cdot\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

where (a) we use Lemma
D.15
, (
9
) in Theorem
B.1
(
A=\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
is the coefficient in front of
\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}
) and Lemma
C.6
.

If
\ell=j
, that is
i\in S_{j}

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{k}{\bm{\mu}}_{k}-\hat{\pi}_{\ell}{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(1-\sum_{k\neq\ell}\frac{\pi_{k}^{*}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{k}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\right)\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{k}{\bm{\mu}}_{k}-\hat{\pi}_{\ell}{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\frac{-1}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\sum_{k\neq\ell}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\pi_{k}^{*}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{k}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{k}{\bm{\mu}}_{k}-\hat{\pi}_{\ell}{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right],

where (a) we use
\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left({\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right)\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\right]=\int_{\bm{x}}\left({\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right)\cdot\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})=0
from Lemma
C.6
. Thus,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{k}{\bm{\mu}}_{k}-\hat{\pi}_{\ell}{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\rangle\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}A}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\cdot\sum_{k\neq\ell}\pi_{k}^{*}\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{k}^{*})}{p_{*}}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{2,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\cdot\pi_{i}\left\|\left\|{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\tau_{i}\right\|_{2,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}A}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\cdot\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/34\right)\cdot\pi_{i}\poly(d)D_{\max}\exp(CD_{\max}B)\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/35\right)\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\cdot\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

where (a) e use Lemma
D.15
, (
9
) in Theorem
B.1
(
A=\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
is the coefficient in front of
\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}
);
(b) we use Lemma
D.15
and Lemma
C.6
.

Combine above with (
9
) in Theorem
B.1
that
\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
, we know
|\hyperlink{termtau2nd12}{\text{\text{(I.ii.1.2)}}}|\lesssim\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/36\right){\varepsilon}
.
∎

Lemma D.9
.

Under Lemma
C.4
, then we have

\displaystyle\hyperlink{termtau2nd13}{\text{\text{(I.ii.1.3)}}}:=\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\sum_{j}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\pi_{k}\widetilde{\tau}_{k}({\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\gtrsim-\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/36\right){\varepsilon}.

Proof.

We focus on each term in
(I.ii.1.3)
indexed by
\ell,i,j,k
in the sum.

If
\ell\neq j
, that is
i\not\in S_{j}

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\pi_{k}\widetilde{\tau}_{k}({\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\pi_{k}\pi_{i}\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*})}{p_{*}}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{2,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left\|\widetilde{\tau}_{k}({\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\right\|_{2,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\pi_{k}\pi_{i}\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/34\right)\cdot\exp(CD_{\max}B)\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\exp(CD_{\max}B)\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/35\right)\cdot\pi_{k}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

where (a) we use Lemma
D.15
and Lemma
C.6
.

If
\ell=j
, that is
i\in S_{j}

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\pi_{k}\widetilde{\tau}_{k}({\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(1-\sum_{s\neq j}\frac{\pi_{s}^{*}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{s}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\right)\pi_{k}\widetilde{\tau}_{k}({\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\geq}}

\displaystyle\frac{-1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}\sum_{s\neq j}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\pi_{s}^{*}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{s}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\pi_{k}\widetilde{\tau}_{k}({\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right],

where (a) we use Lemma
C.6
.

We further have (similar to
\ell\neq j
case)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}}\sum_{s\neq j}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\pi_{s}^{*}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{s}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\pi_{k}\widetilde{\tau}_{k}({\bm{x}})\cdot\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\pi_{i}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\pi_{k}\pi_{i}}{\pi_{j}^{*}}\sum_{s\neq j}\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{s}^{*})}{p_{*}}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{2,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left\|\widetilde{\tau}_{k}({\bm{x}})\tau_{i}({\bm{x}})\right\|_{2,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\frac{\pi_{k}\pi_{i}}{\pi_{\ell}^{*3}}\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/34\right)\cdot\exp(CD_{\max}B)\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\Delta^{2}\exp(CD_{\max}B)\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/35\right)\cdot\pi_{k}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2},

where (a) we use Lemma
D.15
and Lemma
C.6
.

Combine above with (
9
) in Theorem
B.1
that
\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{i}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})
, we know
\hyperlink{termtau2nd13}{\text{\text{(I.ii.1.3)}}}\gtrsim-\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/36\right){\varepsilon}
.
∎

D.4
Bound
(II)

Here we bound
(II)
.

Lemma D.10
.

Under Lemma
C.4
, let
\delta_{\rm close}={\varepsilon}^{1/4}A
with
A=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{mnd}}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{9/4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3/2}D_{\max}^{5}\right)
as (
8
) in Theorem
B.1
, then we have

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termII}{\text{\text{(II)}}}|:=\left|\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}}))\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\right]\right|\lesssim{\varepsilon}^{9/8}.

We further split it into 2 terms as below.

Lemma D.11
.

Under Lemma
C.4
, if
\delta_{\rm close}\leq 1/D_{\max}
, then we have

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termIIi}{\text{}}|:=

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p_{\theta}({\bm{x}}))(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}}))\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})\sqrt{\theta}\delta_{\rm close}{\varepsilon}.

Lemma D.12
.

Under Lemma
C.4
, if
\delta_{\rm close}\leq 1/D_{\max}
, then we have

\displaystyle\hyperlink{termIIii}{\text{}}:=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime\prime}\left((1-t({\bm{x}}))p({\bm{x}})+t({\bm{x}})p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})\right)R({\bm{x}})\cdot(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}}))\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})\frac{\delta_{\rm close}}{\theta^{3/2}}\cdot{\varepsilon}.

Using the above two results, we can give the proof for Lemma
D.10
.

Proof of Lemma
D.10
.

Similar to Lemma
C.5
that bound
(I)
, we are going to expand
\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))
at position
p_{\theta}({\bm{x}}):=\theta p({\bm{x}})+(1-\theta)p^{*}({\bm{x}})
with some
\theta\in(0,1)
. Recall that
p(x)-p_{\theta}(x)=(1-\theta)(p({\bm{x}})-p^{*}({\bm{x}}))=(1-\theta)R({\bm{x}})
, where
R({\bm{x}}):=p({\bm{x}})-p^{*}({\bm{x}})
is the residual. We have (now only expand at 2nd order instead of 3rd order as in Lemma
C.5
)

\displaystyle\ell^{\prime}(p({\bm{x}}))=\ell^{\prime}(p_{\theta}({\bm{x}}))+\ell^{\prime\prime}\left((1-t({\bm{x}}))p({\bm{x}})+t({\bm{x}})p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})\right)\cdot(1-\theta)R({\bm{x}}),

where
t({\bm{x}})\in[0,1]
.

Thus, we further expand
(II)
into following terms:

\displaystyle\hyperlink{termII}{\text{\text{(II)}}}=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime}(p_{\theta}({\bm{x}}))(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}}))\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\right]}_{\hypertarget{termIIi}{\text{(II.i)}}}

\displaystyle+(1-\theta)\underbrace{\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\ell^{\prime\prime}\left((1-t({\bm{x}}))p({\bm{x}})+t({\bm{x}})p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})\right)R({\bm{x}})\cdot(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}}))\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})\right]}_{\hypertarget{termIIii}{\text{(II.ii)}}}.

From Lemma
D.11
and Lemma
D.12
we know

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termIIi}{\text{\text{(II.i)}}}|\lesssim\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})\sqrt{\theta}\delta_{\rm close}{\varepsilon},\quad|\hyperlink{termIIii}{\text{\text{(II.ii)}}}|\lesssim\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})\frac{\delta_{\rm close}}{\theta^{3/2}}\cdot{\varepsilon}.

From (
8
) in Theorem
B.1
we know we can set
\delta_{\rm close}={\varepsilon}^{1/4}A
with
A=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{mnd}}{\pi_{\min}^{*^{9/4}}}\lambda_{\min}^{-3/2}D_{\max}^{5}\right)
. Then choosing
\theta
to be any constant, say
1/2
, since
{\varepsilon}\leq{\varepsilon}_{0}=\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))
, we get
|\hyperlink{termII}{\text{\text{(II)}}}|\lesssim{\varepsilon}^{9/8}
.
∎

D.4.1
Omitted proofs

In this part, we give the omitted proofs for the bound of
(II.i)
and
(II.ii)
.
See
D.11

Proof.

Note that
\ell^{\prime}(u)=-1/u
. We have

\displaystyle\hyperlink{termIIi}{\text{\text{(II.i)}}}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\int_{\bm{x}}\frac{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\cdot(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}}))\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}){\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\int_{\bm{x}}\cdot(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}}))\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}){\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\int_{\bm{x}}\frac{\theta R({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\cdot(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}}))\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}){\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\theta\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\int_{\bm{x}}\frac{R({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\cdot(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}}))\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}){\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}

where (a) we use
\frac{p*({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}=1-\frac{\theta R({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}
; (b) we use
\int_{\bm{x}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}}){\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}=\int_{\bm{x}}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}}){\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}=1
and
\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}(\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*})=0
due to
\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\pi_{i}=\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}
.

We now have

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termIIi}{\text{\text{(II.i)}}}|\leq

\displaystyle\theta\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{|R({\bm{x}})|}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{|\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})|}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}|\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\theta\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}}\right]\right)^{1/2}\cdot\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{p_{*}}\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}-1\right)\right\|_{2,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}|\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\theta\sum_{\ell}\sqrt{\frac{{\varepsilon}}{\theta}}\cdot\frac{1}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}D_{\max}\delta_{\rm close}\cdot\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})\sqrt{\theta}\delta_{\rm close}{\varepsilon}

where (a) we use Claim
C.2
, Lemma
D.13
, (
7
) in Theorem
B.1
and
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\leq\delta_{\rm close}\leq 1/D_{\max}
.
∎

See
D.12

Proof.

Note that
p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})/p({\bm{x}})\geq\theta
. Then recall
\ell^{\prime}(u)=-1/u
, by Lemma
D.16
we know
t({\bm{x}})\geq\frac{1}{2}\theta^{1/2}\left(\frac{p({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{1/2}
and
t({\bm{x}})\in[0,1]
. Using
\ell^{\prime\prime}(u)=1/u^{2}
, we know

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termIIii}{\text{\text{(II.ii)}}}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\frac{1}{\left((1-t({\bm{x}}))p({\bm{x}})+t({\bm{x}})p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})\right)^{2}}|R({\bm{x}})|\cdot|\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})|\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}|\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}|\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\frac{1}{\left(t({\bm{x}})p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})\right)^{2}}|R({\bm{x}})|\cdot|\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})|\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}|\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}|\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\frac{1}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})p({\bm{x}})}\frac{1}{\theta}|R({\bm{x}})|\cdot|\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})|\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}|\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}|\right]

where (a) we use
t({\bm{x}})\in[0,1]
,
p({\bm{x}}),p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})\geq 0
; (b) we use
t({\bm{x}})\geq\frac{1}{2}\theta^{1/2}\left(\frac{p({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{1/2}
.

We further have

\displaystyle|\hyperlink{termIIii}{\text{\text{(II.ii)}}}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\theta}\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}}\left[\frac{|R({\bm{x}})|}{p_{\theta}({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{|\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})|}{p({\bm{x}})}\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}|\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\theta}\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(\delta_{\rm close})}\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{R^{2}({\bm{x}})}{p_{\theta}^{2}({\bm{x}})}\right]\right)^{1/2}\cdot\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{p}\right\|_{4,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}-1\right\|_{4,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\cdot\frac{\pi_{i}}{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}}|\hat{\pi}_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\theta}\sum_{\ell}\sqrt{\frac{{\varepsilon}}{\theta}}\cdot\frac{n}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}D_{\max}\delta_{\rm close}\cdot\sqrt{{\varepsilon}}\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\poly(d,m,n,1/\pi_{\min}^{*},1/\lambda_{\min},D_{\max})\frac{\delta_{\rm close}}{\theta^{3/2}}\cdot{\varepsilon},

where (a) we use Claim
C.2
, Lemma
D.13
, Lemma
D.14
, (
7
) in Theorem
B.1
and
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\leq\delta_{\rm close}\leq 1/D_{\max}
.
∎

D.5
Technical lemma

We collect few technical lemma in this section.

D.5.1
\phi
related bound

This part collects few bound related to
\phi
that will be used to handle cross terms.

Lemma D.13
.

For
i\in S_{\ell}
and constant
\alpha\geq 1
, if
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\leq B\leq D_{\max}
, then there exists constant
C_{1},C_{2},C_{3}
that only depends on
\alpha
such that for
q\in\{p_{*},p\}

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}-1\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}=

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim q}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}-1\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}\leq C_{1}D_{\max}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\exp(C_{2}D_{\max}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}),

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}-1\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p}=

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim q}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}-1\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}\leq C_{1}D_{\max}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\exp(C_{2}D_{\max}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}).

Proof.

We show one by one.

item 1

For
q=p_{*}
, note that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}-1\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}^{\alpha}=

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}-1\right)^{\alpha}\right]=\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle}-1\right)^{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\pi_{j}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim j}\left[\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle+\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle}-1\right)^{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle D_{\max}^{\alpha}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{\alpha}\exp(O(D_{\max}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2})),

where we use Lemma
D.17
.

For
q=p
, similarly note that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}-1\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p}^{\alpha}=

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}-1\right)^{\alpha}\right]=\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p}\left[\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle}-1\right)^{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\pi_{j}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim N({\bm{\mu}}_{j},{\bm{I}})}\left[\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{j}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle+\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle}-1\right)^{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle D_{\max}^{\alpha}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{\alpha}\exp(O(D_{\max}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2})),

where we use Lemma
D.17
.

item 2

For
q=p_{*}
, similarly as item 1,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}-1\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}^{\alpha}=

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}-1\right)^{\alpha}\right]=\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(e^{-\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle}-1\right)^{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\pi_{j}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim j}\left[\left(e^{-\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle}-1\right)^{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle D_{\max}^{\alpha}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{\alpha}\exp(O(D_{\max}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2})),

where we use Lemma
D.17
in last line.

For
q=p
, similarly we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}-1\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p}^{\alpha}=

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}-1\right)^{\alpha}\right]=\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p}\left[\left(e^{-\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle}-1\right)^{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\pi_{j}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim N({\bm{\mu}}_{j},{\bm{I}})}\left[\left(e^{-\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{j}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle-\langle{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j},{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\rangle}-1\right)^{\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle D_{\max}^{\alpha}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{\alpha}\exp(O(D_{\max}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2})),

where we use Lemma
D.17
in last line.
∎

Lemma D.14
.

For
i\in S_{\ell}
and constant
\alpha\geq 1
, if
\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(1/D_{\max})}\pi_{i}\gtrsim\pi_{\ell}^{*}
and
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\leq B\leq D_{\max}
, then there exists constant
C_{1},C_{2},C_{3}
that only depends on
\alpha
such that for
p_{\theta}=\theta p+(1-\theta)p_{*}
with any
\theta\in[0,1]

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{p}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}=

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}\leq C_{1}n/\pi_{\ell}^{*},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}=

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}\leq C_{2}\frac{n}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\left(1+\exp(C_{3}D_{\max}B)\right),

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}=

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}\leq 1/\pi_{\ell}^{*},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}=

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}\leq C_{2}\frac{1}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\left(1+\exp(C_{3}D_{\max}B)\right).

Proof.

Denote
\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}:=\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(1/D_{\max})}\pi_{i}
. We know
\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}\gtrsim\pi_{\ell}^{*}
. It suffices to first consider
{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}
with
\theta=0,1
(that is
{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}
or
{\bm{x}}\sim p
), and then give the bound for
\theta\in[0,1]
.

item 1

For
q\in\{p_{*},p\}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{p}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(1/D_{\max})}\pi_{i}(\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i}))}{p}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}+\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(1/D_{\max})}\left\|\frac{\pi_{i}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(1/D_{\max})}\frac{\pi_{i}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p}\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}-1\right)\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}+n

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}(1/D_{\max})}\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}-1\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}+n\lesssim n,

where last line is due to Lemma
D.13
and
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|\leq 1/D_{\max}
.

This implies for
q\in\{p_{*},p\}

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{p}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}=\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim q}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}\lesssim n/\pi_{\ell}^{*},

where we use the fact that
\widetilde{\pi}_{\ell}\gtrsim\pi_{\ell}
. Hence, for
p_{\theta}
with any
\theta\in[0,1]

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}=\left(\theta\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]+(1-\theta)\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}\lesssim n/\pi_{\ell}^{*},

item 2

For
q\in\{p_{*},p\}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{p}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}+\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{p}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}+\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{p}\right\|_{2\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}\left\|\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})/\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})-1\right\|_{2\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{n}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}+\frac{n}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}D_{\max}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\exp(O(D_{\max}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}))\leq\frac{n}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\left(1+\exp(O(D_{\max}B))\right)

where we use Lemma
D.13
and item 1 in the last line.

Hence, for
p_{\theta}
with any
\theta\in[0,1]

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{\theta}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}=

\displaystyle\left(\theta\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]+(1-\theta)\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{n}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\left(1+\exp(O(D_{\max}B))\right)

item 3

This directly follows from
\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})/p_{*}({\bm{x}})\leq 1/\pi_{\ell}^{*}
.

item 4

Similar to item 2, for
q\in\{p_{*},p\}
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}+\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})-\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}+\frac{1}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\left\|\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})/\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})-1\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim q}\lesssim\frac{1}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\left(1+\exp(O(D_{\max}B))\right),

where we use item 3 and Lemma
D.13
.

Then follow the same argument as in item 2, we get the desired result for
p_{\theta}
with any
\theta\in[0,1]
.
∎

Lemma D.15
.

For
i\in S_{\ell}
,
j\neq\ell
and constant
\alpha\geq 1
, if
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\leq B\leq D_{\max}
and
\Delta^{2}\geq CD_{\max}B
for a large enough constant
C
that only depends on
\alpha
, then there exists a constant
C_{1}
that only depending on
\alpha
such that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*})}{p_{*}}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}=

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}\leq\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/8\alpha\right),

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*})}{p_{*}}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}=

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\right)^{1/\alpha}\leq C_{1}\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/17\alpha\right).

Proof.

We show this one by one.

item 1

We have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*})}{p_{*}}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}^{\alpha}=\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\left(\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\alpha}\right]\leq\frac{1}{\left(\pi_{j}^{*}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\right)^{\alpha-1}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\right].

We further have

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left[\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}\right]=

\displaystyle\int_{\bm{x}}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}{\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\exp\left(-\frac{1}{8}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\int_{\bm{x}}\phi\left(\frac{{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}+{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}}{2};{\bm{x}}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}}\sqrt{\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*};{\bm{x}})}{p_{*}({\bm{x}})}}{\textnormal{d}}{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/8\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi_{j}^{*}\pi_{\ell}^{*}}}

where (a) we use
\exp\left(-\frac{1}{4}\left\|{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{1}{4}\left\|{\bm{x}}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)=\exp\left(-\frac{1}{8}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{x}}-\frac{{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}+{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}}{2}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)
.

Together above we get the desired result.

item 2

We have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*})}{p_{*}}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}=

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*})}{p_{*}}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{p_{*}}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}\right\|_{\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\leq\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*})}{p_{*}}\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}{p_{*}}\right\|_{2\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}\right\|_{2\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/16\alpha\right)\cdot\left(\left\|\frac{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i})}{\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})}-1\right\|_{2\alpha,{\bm{x}}\sim p_{*}}+1\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{\lesssim}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/16\alpha\right)\cdot D_{\max}B\exp(O(D_{\max}B))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi_{j}^{*}\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\exp\left(-\Delta^{2}/17\alpha\right)

where (a) we use item 1;
(b) we use Lemma
D.13
;
(c) we use
\Delta^{2}\geq CD_{\max}B
for large enough constant
C
that depending on
\alpha
.

∎

D.5.2
Other Technical Lemma

The following lemma is essentially a Taylor expansion of
-1/x
with a careful control of the high-order error.

Lemma D.16
.

Given
x\neq y>0
, we have

-\frac{1}{x}=-\frac{1}{y}+\frac{1}{y^{2}}(x-y)-\frac{1}{2}\frac{2}{((1-t)x+ty)^{3}}(x-y)^{2},\quad\text{with }t=\frac{x^{\frac{1}{3}}(y^{\frac{2}{3}}-x^{\frac{2}{3}})}{y-x}\in[0,1].

Moreover, for
a\leq 1

t\geq\frac{2}{3}a^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(\frac{x}{y}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}\quad\text{if }\frac{y}{x}\geq a.

Also we have

-\frac{1}{x}=-\frac{1}{y}+\frac{1}{((1-t)x+ty)^{2}}(x-y),\quad\text{with }t=\frac{x^{\frac{1}{2}}(y^{\frac{1}{2}}-x^{\frac{1}{2}})}{y-x}\in[0,1].

Moreover, for
a\leq 1

t\geq\frac{1}{2}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{x}{y}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\quad\text{if }\frac{y}{x}\geq a.

Proof.

We solve the following to get
t

-\frac{1}{x}=-\frac{1}{y}+\frac{1}{y^{2}}(x-y)-\frac{1}{2}\frac{2}{((1-t)x+ty)^{3}}(x-y)^{2}.

We have

\frac{1}{((1-t)x+ty)^{3}}(x-y)^{2}=\frac{y-x}{xy}+\frac{1}{y^{2}}(x-y)=\frac{(x-y)^{2}}{xy^{2}}\qquad\Rightarrow\qquad(xy^{2})^{\frac{1}{3}}=x+t(y-x).

Thus,

t=\frac{(xy^{2})^{\frac{1}{3}}-x}{y-x}=\frac{x^{\frac{1}{3}}(y^{\frac{2}{3}}-x^{\frac{2}{3}})}{y-x}

Let
g(z)=(z^{2}-1)/(z^{3}-1)
. We can see that
t=g((y/x)^{1/3})
. It is not hard to show
g(z)\in(0,1)
for
z>0
. Also, when
z=(y/x)^{1/3}\geq a^{1/3}
, we can show
g(z)\geq\frac{2}{3}\frac{a^{1/3}}{z}
when
a\leq 1
.

Similarly, for the second one
we solve the following to get
t

-\frac{1}{x}=-\frac{1}{y}+\frac{1}{((1-t)x+ty)^{2}}(x-y).

We have

\frac{1}{((1-t)x+ty)^{2}}(x-y)=\frac{x-y}{xy}\qquad\Rightarrow\qquad(xy)^{\frac{1}{2}}=x+t(y-x).

Thus,

t=\frac{(xy)^{\frac{1}{2}}-x}{y-x}=\frac{x^{\frac{1}{2}}(y^{\frac{1}{2}}-x^{\frac{1}{2}})}{y-x}.

Using similar argument we get
t\geq\frac{1}{2}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{x}{y}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\quad\text{if }\frac{y}{x}\geq a.

∎

The following lemma can be viewed as a Taylor expansion of
e^{a+bx}
.

Lemma D.17
.

Let
\alpha\geq 1
. Then for all real
a,b
, there exists constants
C
(depending only on
\alpha
) such that

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{x\sim N(0,1)}\bigl[(e^{a+bx}-1)^{\alpha}\bigr]\leq

\displaystyle C(|a|^{\alpha}+|b|^{\alpha})\exp\bigl(\alpha|a|+\alpha^{2}b^{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{x\sim N(0,1)}\bigl[(e^{a+bx}-1-a-bx)^{\alpha}\bigr]\leq

\displaystyle C(|a|^{2\alpha}+|b|^{2\alpha})\exp(\alpha|a|+\alpha^{2}b^{2}).

Proof.

We deal with
\mathbf{E}[(e^{a+bx}-1)^{\alpha}]
and
\mathbf{E}[(e^{a+bx}-1-a-bx)^{\alpha}]
separately. Denote
t=a+bx
.

Bound
\mathbf{E}[(e^{a+bx}-1)^{\alpha}]

By Taylor’s theorem with remainder we know

\bigl|e^{t}-1\bigr|\leq te^{|t|}.

Thus

\mathbf{E}\bigl[(e^{t}-1)^{\alpha}\bigr]\leq\mathbf{E}\bigl[|t|^{\alpha}e^{\alpha|t|}\bigr]=\mathbf{E}\bigl[|a+bx|^{\alpha}e^{\alpha|t|}\bigr]\leq 2^{\alpha-1}\mathbf{E}\bigl[\bigl(|a|^{\alpha}+|bx|^{\alpha}\bigr)e^{\alpha|t|}\bigr].

Since
|t|\leq|a|+|b||x|
,

\mathbf{E}[e^{\alpha|t|}]\leq e^{\alpha|a|}\mathbf{E}[e^{\alpha|b||x|}]\leq 2e^{\alpha|a|}\mathbf{E}[e^{\alpha bx}]=2e^{\alpha|a|+\tfrac{1}{2}\alpha^{2}b^{2}}.

By Cauchy–Schwarz,

\mathbf{E}\bigl[|x|^{\alpha}e^{\alpha|t|}\bigr]\leq\sqrt{\mathbf{E}[x^{2\alpha}]}\sqrt{\mathbf{E}[e^{2\alpha|t|}]}\leq\sqrt{M_{2\alpha}}\,\sqrt{2}\,e^{\alpha|a|+\alpha^{2}b^{2}},

where
M_{2\alpha}=\mathbf{E}[x^{2\alpha}]
.

Thus, combining above we have

\mathbf{E}\bigl[(e^{t}-1)^{\alpha}\bigr]\leq 2^{\alpha-1}\Bigl(|a|^{\alpha}\cdot 2e^{\alpha|a|+\tfrac{1}{2}\alpha^{2}b^{2}}+|b|^{\alpha}\cdot\sqrt{2M_{2\alpha}}e^{\alpha|a|+\alpha^{2}b^{2}}\Bigr)\lesssim(|a|^{\alpha}+|b|^{\alpha})\exp(\alpha|a|+\alpha^{2}b^{2}).

Bound
\mathbf{E}[(e^{a+bx}-1-a-bx)^{\alpha}]

By Taylor’s theorem with remainder we know

\bigl|e^{t}-1-t\bigr|\leq\frac{t^{2}}{2}e^{|t|}.

Thus

\mathbf{E}\bigl[(e^{t}-1-t)^{\alpha}\bigr]\leq 2^{-\alpha}\mathbf{E}\bigl[|t|^{2\alpha}e^{\alpha|t|}\bigr]=2^{-\alpha}\mathbf{E}\bigl[|a+bx|^{2\alpha}e^{\alpha|t|}\bigr]\leq 2^{\alpha-1}\mathbf{E}\bigl[\bigl(|a|^{2\alpha}+|b\,x|^{2\alpha}\bigr)e^{\alpha|t|}\bigr].

Since
|t|\leq|a|+|b||x|
,

\mathbf{E}[e^{\alpha|t|}]\leq e^{\alpha|a|}\mathbf{E}[e^{\alpha|b||x|}]\leq 2e^{\alpha|a|}\mathbf{E}[e^{\alpha bx}]=2e^{\alpha|a|+\tfrac{1}{2}\alpha^{2}b^{2}}.

By Cauchy–Schwarz,

\mathbf{E}\bigl[|x|^{2\alpha}e^{\alpha|t|}\bigr]\leq\sqrt{\mathbf{E}[x^{4\alpha}]}\sqrt{\mathbf{E}[e^{2\alpha|t|}]}\leq\sqrt{M_{4\alpha}}\,\sqrt{2}\,e^{\alpha|a|+\alpha^{2}b^{2}},

where
M_{4\alpha}=\mathbf{E}[x^{4\alpha}]
.

Thus, combining above we have

\mathbf{E}\bigl[(e^{t}-1-t)^{\alpha}\bigr]\leq 2^{\alpha-1}\Bigl(|a|^{2\alpha}\cdot 2e^{\alpha|a|+\tfrac{1}{2}\alpha^{2}b^{2}}+|b|^{2\alpha}\cdot\sqrt{2M_{4\alpha}}e^{\alpha|a|+\alpha^{2}b^{2}}\Bigr)\lesssim(|a|^{2\alpha}+|b|^{2\alpha})\exp(\alpha|a|+\alpha^{2}b^{2}).

∎

Appendix E
Global convergence

The goal of this section is to show within polynomial time that the loss will be reduced to below certain threshold
{\varepsilon}_{0}
that would allow us to do local convergence analysis later in Appendix
C
.

Initialization

The lemma below shows some properities of initialization that we will use in the later analysis.

Lemma E.1
.

Under random initialization that
{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\overset{\text{i.i.d.}}{\sim}p_{*}
, if number of components
n\gtrsim\frac{1}{\pi_{\min}^{*}}\log m
, with high probability at least
1-1/\poly(m)
, we have

1.

For each cluster
\ell
, there exists at least one component
{\bm{\mu}}_{i}
such that
{\bm{\mu}}_{i}\in S_{\ell}
(i.e.,
S_{\ell}\neq\phi
) and
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\lesssim\sqrt{d}
.

2.

B^{2}:=U({\bm{\mu}}^{(0)})\lesssim dn

3.

Initial loss
\mathcal{L}^{(0)}\lesssim\log(n)+d

Proof.

We show one-by one.

item 1

Note that sample from
p_{*}
can be viewed as first randomly choose from
j\sim\{1,\ldots,m\}
with probability
\pi_{1}^{*},\ldots,\pi_{m}^{*}
and then sample from
N({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},{\bm{I}})
. Then the result follows from coupon collector’s problem and standard concentration for Gaussian.

item 2

Recall
U({\bm{\mu}})=\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}
. Then it directly follows from item 1.

item 3

From item 1 we know
\hat{\pi}_{\ell}\geq 1/n
and note that
\pi_{i}/\hat{\pi}_{\ell}\leq 1
, so by Lemma
E.9
we know

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}\leq\sum_{\ell}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\log\frac{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\hat{\pi}_{\ell}}+\sum_{\ell}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{i}\frac{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\hat{\pi}_{\ell}}\frac{1}{2}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\log(n)+d

∎

E.1
Proof of main result for global phase Theorem
4.2

Now we are ready to state the main result for the global convergence phase that ends with loss
\mathcal{L}\leq{\varepsilon}_{0}
for certain threshold
{\varepsilon}_{0}=\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))
.

See
4.2

The key is to show the following descent direction result. The proof is deferred to Appendix
E.2
.

Lemma E.2
.

Under Theorem
E.3
,
then for
t\leq T_{1}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}\rangle\gtrsim\min\left\{\frac{1}{d},\mathcal{L}^{2}\right\}.

Once having the above result, we can get the gradient norm lower bound.

Theorem E.3
.

Under Assumption
3
,
2
,
1
, if loss
\mathcal{L}\leq\mathcal{L}^{(0)}\lesssim d
,
\bm{\pi}
is near optimal in the sense that

\pi_{i}\left|\nabla_{\pi_{i}}\mathcal{L}+1\right|=\left|\mathbf{E}_{\bm{x}}\left[\frac{\pi_{i}\phi({\bm{\mu}}_{i};{\bm{x}})}{p({\bm{x}})}\right]-\pi_{i}\right|\leq{\varepsilon}_{\pi}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{0}^{2}/(D_{\max}^{2}+B^{4}),

U({\bm{\mu}})\leq B^{2}
, then there exists a threshold
{\varepsilon}_{0}=\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))
and a constant
c_{1}
such that when
\mathcal{L}\geq{\varepsilon}_{0}
(i.e.,
t\leq T_{1}
) we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\mu}}\mathcal{L}\right\|_{F}\geq\frac{c_{1}}{B}\min\left\{\frac{1}{d},\mathcal{L}^{2}\right\}.

Moreover, the descent direction can also imply the following regularity condition that
U({\bm{\mu}})
remains bounded.

Theorem E.4
.

Under Theorem
4.2
, we have for any
t\leq T_{1}
,
U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\leq B^{2}
.

Now we can give the proof for Theorem
4.2
.

Proof of Theorem
4.2
.

By Lemma
F.2
, Theorem
E.4
and Theorem
E.3
we know

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})-\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})\leq-\frac{\eta}{2}\left\|\nabla\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})\right\|_{F}^{2}\lesssim-\frac{\eta}{B}\min\left\{\frac{1}{d},\mathcal{L}^{2}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})\right\}.

We know it takes at most
\mathcal{O}\left(Bd^{2}/\eta\right)
for loss
\mathcal{L}^{2}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t)})\lesssim 1/d
. After that, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})-\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})\lesssim-\frac{\eta}{B}\mathcal{L}^{2}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)}).

Note that by Lemma
F.2
we know
\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})\leq\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})
, therefore (in the equation below, we omit the dependency on
\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)}
as all of them depends on it)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})}-\frac{1}{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})}=\frac{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})-\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})}{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})}\geq\frac{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})-\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})}{\mathcal{L}^{2}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})}\geq\eta/B.

This implies

\frac{1}{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})}-\frac{1}{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t)})}\geq\frac{1}{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})}-\frac{1}{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t+1)})}\geq\eta/B.

Therefore, we have

\frac{1}{\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)},\bm{\pi}^{(t)})}\geq\frac{\eta t}{B}+\sqrt{d}.

This implies
T_{1}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{Bd^{2}}{\eta}+\frac{B}{\eta{\varepsilon}_{0}}\right)
.

∎

E.2
Proof of Lemma
E.2

In this part, we give the proof for Lemma
E.2
.
See
E.2

As discussed in Section
4.2
, a key lemma is the following that lower bound
\bm{\widetilde{\psi}}_{\ell}({\bm{x}})
.

Lemma E.5
.

Denote
\bm{\widetilde{\psi}}_{\ell}({\bm{x}}):=\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})
. Under Lemma
E.2
, we have for any
\ell\in[m]

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\|\bm{\widetilde{\psi}}_{\ell}({\bm{x}})\right\|^{2}\right]\gtrsim\min\Bigg\{\frac{1}{d},

\displaystyle d\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\frac{\pi_{i}}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left(\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\frac{\pi_{i}}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{2}-\left(\mathcal{O}\left(D_{\max}^{2}+B^{4}\right){\varepsilon}_{\pi}+\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))\right)\Bigg\}.

The following further relates the above RHS to loss
\mathcal{L}
.

Lemma E.6
.

If
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\leq B\leq D_{\max}
for all
i,\ell
, loss
\mathcal{L}\leq\mathcal{L}^{(0)}\lesssim d
,
\Delta\geq C\max\{\sqrt{D_{\max}B},\sqrt{d/\pi_{\min}^{*}}\}
with large enough constant
C
and
\bm{\pi}
is near optimal in the sense that

\left|\nabla_{\pi_{i}}\mathcal{L}+1\right|\leq{\varepsilon}_{\pi},

then

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{2}\leq\sum_{\ell}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\left(\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\frac{\pi_{i}}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left(\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\frac{\pi_{i}}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{2}\right)+\left(n{\varepsilon}_{\pi}+\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))\right)

Now we are ready to give the proof for Lemma
E.2
.

Proof of Lemma
E.2
.

We start with handling
\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}
given in Lemma
A.2
.

\displaystyle\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\sum_{j\in[m]}\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\sum_{j\in[m]}\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*})\right]+\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\sum_{j\in[m]}\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})\right]

Therefore we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\|\sum_{j\in[m]}\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\langle\sum_{p\in[m]}\sum_{i\in S_{p}}\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{p}^{*}),\sum_{j\in[m]}\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})\right\rangle\right].

For the second term above

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\langle\sum_{p\in[m]}\sum_{i\in S_{p}}\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{p}^{*}),\sum_{j\in[m]}\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})\right\rangle\right]\right|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle B\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\sum_{j\in[m]}\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}})\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle BD_{\max}\sum_{\ell\neq j\in[m]}\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\psi_{k}({\bm{x}})\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{\lesssim}}BD_{\max}\sum_{\ell\neq j\in[m]}\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\frac{\pi_{k}n}{\sqrt{\hat{\pi}_{j}\hat{\pi}_{\ell}}}\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{c})}}{{\lesssim}}\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))

where (a) we use
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{p}^{*}\right\|
for
i\in S_{p}
;
(b) we use Lemma
E.7
;
(c) we use
\hat{\pi}_{\ell}\gtrsim\pi_{\ell}^{*}\geq\pi_{\min}^{*}
from Lemma
E.10
.

For the first term above, denote
\bm{\widetilde{\psi}}_{\ell}({\bm{x}}):=\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})
, we further have

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\|\sum_{j\in[m]}\sum_{k\in S_{j}}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*})\right\|^{2}\right]=\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\|\sum_{j\in[m]}\bm{\widetilde{\psi}}_{j}({\bm{x}})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\|\bm{\widetilde{\psi}}_{\ell}({\bm{x}})\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\sum_{j,k\in[m],(j,k)\neq(\ell,\ell)}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\langle\bm{\widetilde{\psi}}_{j}({\bm{x}}),\bm{\widetilde{\psi}}_{k}({\bm{x}})\rangle\right].

Note that the second term above for
(j,k)\neq(\ell,\ell)
(WLOG, assume
j\neq\ell
):

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\langle\bm{\widetilde{\psi}}_{j}({\bm{x}}),\bm{\widetilde{\psi}}_{k}({\bm{x}})\rangle\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\leq}}B^{2}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{\lesssim}}\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2})),

where (a) we use
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\leq B
for
i\in S_{\ell}
and all
\ell
; (b) we use Lemma
E.7
and Lemma
E.10
.

Therefore, combine all above we get with some constant
C

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}\rangle\geq\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\|\bm{\widetilde{\psi}}_{\ell}({\bm{x}})\right\|^{2}\right]-\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\geq}}

\displaystyle\min\left\{\tfrac{1}{d},\sum_{\ell}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\left(d\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\frac{\pi_{i}}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left(\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\frac{\pi_{i}}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{2}\right)\right\}-\left(\mathcal{O}\left(D_{\max}^{2}+B^{4}\right){\varepsilon}_{\pi}+\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{\geq}}

\displaystyle\min\left\{\tfrac{1}{d},\mathcal{L}^{2}\right\}-\left(\mathcal{O}\left(D_{\max}^{2}+B^{4}\right){\varepsilon}_{\pi}+\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))\right)

where (a) we use Lemma
E.5
to lower bound
\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\|\bm{\widetilde{\psi}}_{\ell}({\bm{x}})\right\|^{2}\right]
;
(b) we use Lemma
E.6
to relate with loss
\mathcal{L}
.

Properly set
{\varepsilon}_{0}^{2}\geq\mathcal{O}\left(D_{\max}^{2}+B^{4}\right){\varepsilon}_{\pi}+\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))
and
{\varepsilon}_{\pi}
to be small enough we know for
\mathcal{L}\geq{\varepsilon}_{0}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}\rangle\gtrsim\min\left\{\tfrac{1}{d},\mathcal{L}^{2}\right\}.

∎

E.3
Omitted proofs in Section
E.1
and Section
E.2

The lemma shows
U({\bm{\mu}})
remains bounded. The proof relies on descent direction lemma (Lemma
E.2
).
See
E.4

Proof.

By Lemma
F.2
we have

\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})\leq\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\leq\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(0)})\lesssim d.

Then by Lemma
E.2
we have (following is the same as in the proof of Theorem
C.2
)

\displaystyle U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})

\displaystyle=U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})-\eta\sum_{j}\sum_{i\in S_{j}}\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{i}^{(t)}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*},\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\rangle+\eta^{2}\sum_{i\in[n]}\left\|\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})-\frac{\eta\mathcal{L}}{4}+\eta^{2}\sum_{i\in[n]}\left\|\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\right\|_{2}^{2}.

Note that

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[n]}\left\|\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\right\|_{2}^{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[n]}\left\|\sum_{j\in[m]}\pi_{j}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim j}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\sum_{k\in[n]}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{j}^{*})\right]\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[n]}\left\|\sum_{j\in[m]}\pi_{j}^{*}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim j}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})\right]\right\|_{2}^{2}+D_{\max}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})+D_{\max}^{2}

Thus, when
\eta\lesssim{\varepsilon}/D_{\max}^{2}
we know

\displaystyle U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t+1)})\leq U({\bm{\mu}}^{(t)})\leq U({\bm{\mu}}^{(0)})\leq B^{2}.

∎

Combining norm bound (Theorem
E.4
) and descent direction (Lemma
E.2
) we get the following gradient norm lower bound.
See
E.3

Proof.

From Lemma
E.2
we know

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}\rangle\gtrsim\min\left\{\frac{1}{d},\mathcal{L}^{2}\right\}.

Note that

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\langle{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*},\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}\rangle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{\ell\in[m]}\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\left\|{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\left\|\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}\right\|_{F}=U^{1/2}({\bm{\mu}})\left\|\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}\right\|_{F}\leq B\left\|\nabla_{{\bm{\mu}}_{i}}\mathcal{L}\right\|_{F}.

Combine the above we get the result.
∎

The following result, as discussed in Section
4.2
, uses the idea of Hermite expansion of
\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})
.
See
E.5

Proof.

WLOG, we may assume
{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}=0
since we can shift all by
{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}
. From Lemma
E.10
we know
\hat{\pi}_{\ell}\gtrsim\pi_{\ell}^{*}
for all
\ell
, so we can use Lemma
E.8
in below.

We can write
\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})
(
i\in S_{\ell}
) in terms of Hermite expansion (see more introduction of Hermite polynomial in Appendix
A.3
)

\displaystyle\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})=\sum_{k\geq 0}\langle\bm{\hat{\psi}}_{i,k},\He_{k}({\bm{x}})\rangle,\quad\text{with }\bm{\hat{\psi}}_{i,k}=\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\He_{k}({\bm{x}})]

Notably, we have
\He_{0}({\bm{x}})=1
and
\He_{1}({\bm{x}})={\bm{x}}
so

\displaystyle\bm{\hat{\psi}}_{i,0}=\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\approx}}\frac{\pi_{i}}{\pi_{\ell}^{*}},\quad\bm{\hat{\psi}}_{i,1}=\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}}){\bm{x}}]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{b})}}{{=}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}[\nabla_{\bm{x}}\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})]=\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k})]

where (a) we use Lemma
E.8
(we will make it precise later); (b) we use Stein’s lemma (Lemma
A.1
).

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\|\bm{\widetilde{\psi}}_{\ell}({\bm{x}})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]=\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\sum_{k\geq 0}\langle\bm{\hat{\psi}}_{i,k},\He_{k}({\bm{x}})\rangle({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k\geq 0}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\langle\bm{\hat{\psi}}_{i,k},\He_{k}({\bm{x}})\rangle({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\bm{\hat{\psi}}_{i,0}({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\bm{\hat{\psi}}_{i,1}({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})^{\top}\right\|_{F}^{2}

where we use the Hermite expansion of
\psi_{i}
above and only keep the 0th and 1st order of Hermite term (recall
\He_{0}({\bm{x}})=1
and
\He_{1}({\bm{x}})={\bm{x}}
).

For 0th order term, we have

\displaystyle\pi_{\ell}^{*2}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\bm{\hat{\psi}}_{i,0}({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{\ell}^{*}\bm{\hat{\psi}}_{i,0}{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{a})}}{{\geq}}

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\pi_{i}{\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\pi_{\ell}^{*}{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}-C\pi_{\ell}D_{\max}^{2}\left(n{\varepsilon}_{\pi}+\pi_{\ell}^{*}\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))\right)

where (a) we use Lemma
E.8
, Lemma
E.10
and
C
is some constant.

For 1st order term, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\bm{\hat{\psi}}_{i,1}({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})^{\top}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\right)\right]({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*})^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\right)\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\right)^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\right)\left(\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right)^{\top}\right].

For the second term, we have (it is a cross term in the sense that
k\not\in S_{\ell})

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\right)\left(\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right)^{\top}\right]\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\sum_{k\not\in S_{\ell}}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\cdot\left(\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right)^{\top}\right]\right\|_{F}\lesssim\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2})),

where we use Lemma
E.7
and Lemma
E.10
.

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\right)\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\right)^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right)\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right)^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle-\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\sum_{k\not\in S_{\ell}}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\cdot\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\right)^{\top}\right]-\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\right)\cdot\sum_{k\not\in S_{\ell}}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\left({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\right)\left({\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}-\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\right)^{\top}\right].

Thus, using Lemma
E.7
and Lemma
E.10
on the second line term (again it’s a cross term) we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\right)\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-\sum_{k}\psi_{k}({\bm{x}}){\bm{\mu}}_{k}\right)^{\top}\right]\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\in S_{\ell}}\mathbf{E}_{{\bm{x}}\sim\ell}\left[\psi_{i}({\bm{x}})\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right)\left({\bm{\mu}}_{i}-{\bm{\mu}}_{\ell}^{*}\right)^{\top}\right]\right\|_{F}-\exp(-\Theta(\Delta^{2}))
